toṉmic cemmoḻi
tamiḻ
pi. irāmanātaṉ ka.mu., ca.i.
patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam



nūṟ kuṟippu



poruḷaṭakkam

aṇinturi
tamiḻ muntu cemmoḻi eṉpati nilināṭṭum vakiyil ataṉ toṉmiyiyum paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappukaḷiyum ēraṇa neṟippaṭi eṭuttu viḷakki aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'toṉmic cemmoḻi tamiḻ eṉṉum innūl cemmoḻi paṟṟiyum tamiḻiṉ etirkālam paṟṟiyum palvēṟu pulaṉaṅkaḷit tāṅki yuḷḷatu.
nūlāciriyar pi. irāmanātaṉ moḻiyiyal, varalāṟu, akaḻvāyvu mutaliya tuṟikaḷil āḻaṅkāṟpaṭṭavar; tamiḻiyam taḻittaṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa eḻuttup paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭiruppavar. 'cintuveḷit tol tamiḻ nākarikam A New Account of the History and Culture of the Tamils' eṉṉun tamiḻ, āṅkila āyvu nūlkaḷi eḻuti veḷi yiṭṭirukkum avar, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar avarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ talimiyi nāṭṭum coṟkaḷ' eṉṉun talippil 'centamiḻc celviyil 1977-80il eḻuti vanta toṭarkaṭṭurikaḷi (talimit tamiḻ eṉṉum peyaril tamiḻmaṇ patippakam taṉi nūlāka veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷa tu)"Nostratics : The Light from Tamil According to Devaneyan '' eṉṉum peyaril āṅkilattil moḻiyākkam ceytu paṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷiṉ kavaṉatti īrttiruppavar; pāvāṇar peritum pārāṭṭum varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñarkaḷāṉa vi. ār. irāmaccantiraṉār, piṭi cīṉivācaṉār ākiyōr Originad Spread of Tamils ' eṉavum His - ory of the Tamils ' eṉavum āṅkilattil eḻutiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷukku irāmanātaṉ ceytuḷḷa tamiḻākkamāṉa tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum', 'tamiḻar varalāṟu' eṉṉum nūlkaḷum, cōviyattu aṟiñar alekcāṇṭar kāntiratāv eḻutiya mupperuṅkaṭaṟputirkaḷ (Riddles of Three Oceans ) eṉṉum āyvu nūlil kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭuk kōṭpāṭṭukku araṇ ceyyum vakiyil amintuḷḷa intiyap peruṅkaṭal paṟṟiya ataṉ iṭippakutiyiṉ tamiḻākkamāṉa avartam 'tamiḻa nākarikamum cintuveḷi nākarikamum - teṉperuṅkaṭal āyvukaḷ' eṉṉum nūlum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka vāyilāka veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.
avvariciyil oṉṟāṉa innūl tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭattiṉ iṉṟiya ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ' eṉpatu mutal 'tamiḻiṉ etirkālam - oru tolinōkku' eṉpatu īṟāṉa ēḻu iyalkaḷit tāṅkiyuḷḷatu.
kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷiṉ paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cevvilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟum ammoḻikaḷic cemmoḻikaḷ eṉṟum aḻitta airōppiyarkaḷ cīṉam, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpiyam ākiya moḻikaḷiyum avvāṟē karutiyatōṭu, 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil veḷivanta paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ kirēkka ilattīṉa camaṟkirutat tol ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkuc camamāṉa niliyil - cila kūṟukaḷil avaṟṟiyum viñcum niliyil uḷḷati uṇarntatum - 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilēyē tamiḻic cemmoḻiyākak koṇṭaṉa rēṉum, tamiḻ aṟiñar perumakkaḷum amippukaḷ cilavum tamiḻic cem moḻiyāka ēṟṟut takka muṟiyil pōṟṟumāṟu kural eḻuppiyapōtilum, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkeṉa akkaṟi yōṭu poruḷutavi ceytu ūkkuvitta intiya aracu tamiḻik kaṇṭu koḷḷavē illi.
tamiḻnāṭṭil nayaṉmik kaṭciyilum pērāyak kaṭciyilum mutaṉmi peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ tamiḻarallātār ātalāl akkaṭcikaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattil tamiḻic cemmoḻiyāka ēṟṟu aṟivikkumāṟu intiya aracukkuk kōrikkitāṉum vittilar; makkaḷ amippukaḷum kaṇṭu koḷḷavilli. (tamiḻarkaḷ cilar poṟuppēṟpiṉum anniliyē toṭarntatu.) eṉṉum varalāṟṟu uṇmiyi irāmanātaṉ kūrntu nōkki innūlil tōṟṟuvāyilēyē teḷivākak kūṟiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappu naṭuvam mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa eḻuvakip paṇikaḷum teḷivuṟa eṭutturikkap paṭṭiruppatōṭu, tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpatu kuṟittu kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka mutalē aṟiñarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya karutturikaḷum, amippukaḷ eḻuppiya kōrikkikaḷum, piṟavum tokuttaḷikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kālṭuvel kālam vari tamiḻiya (Tamulian ) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē vaḻaṅki vanta peyarit tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiyavar avarē eṉpatik kuṟippiṭum irāmanātaṉ, tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccit tiripu vaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatit teḷivuṟuttuvatōṭu ē. ci. paruṉel intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil ( 1872) kumārilapaṭṭiriṉ tantiravārttikattil kaṇṭa 'appaṭit tamiḻ maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil' eṉap poruḷpaṭum 'tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva' eṉṉum vācakattip paṭumōcamāka 'āntra tirāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva eṉṟu acciṭṭattiyum at tavaṟāṉa vācakattiyē kālṭuvel, teṉkōṉā pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar eṉpatiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭukiṟār. aṉṟiyum itu tavaṟāṉatu eṉpati intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42 ām tokutiyil (1913) pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaṉār teḷivuṟutti niṟuviya piṉṉum, ''kumāril paṭṭar tamiḻiyum teluṅkiyum oru cērak kuṟittār' eṉṟu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palar tavaṟākavē eḻuti varuvatic cuṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tolkāppiyamum kaḻaka (caṅka) ilakkiyaṅkaḷum veḷippaṭātirunta kālattilēyē ellicutturiyum kālṭuvelum tam ārāycci vaṉmi yāl tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum piṟa ciṟappukaḷiyum kaṇṭurittatiyum ap paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ veḷivantu paraviya niliyil tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpati moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭatiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭum āciriyar, kaḻakam paṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappukaḷ kuṟittum paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷa karuttukaḷit tokuttut tantiruppatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.
cemmoḻit tamiḻ nūlkaḷip piṟamoḻikaḷil peyarttal eṉṉum pakutiyil, ilakkiyaṅkaḷi moḻipeyarppatil uḷḷa iṭarppāṭukaḷik kūṟappukuntu, 'tirukkuṟaḷi moḻipeyarttal eṉṉum nūlil kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi āṅkilattil moḻipeyarppatil uḷḷa cikkalkaḷi viḷakkum pakutiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu moḻipeyarppup paṇiyil īṭupaṭuvōriṉ taṉik kavaṉattiṟku uriyatākum.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyic cintuveḷi nākarika akaḻvāyvu, annākarika eḻuttāyvu uḷḷiṭṭa piṟa aṟivup pulaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇinta āyviṉ mūlam niṟuvuvatum palvēṟu aṟiñarkaḷiṉ āyvurikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkiyiruppatum āciriyariṉ parantupaṭṭa nūlaṟiviyum nuṇmatiyiyum teḷḷat teḷiya veḷippaṭuttuvaṉavākum.
tamiḻ, tamiḻarp paṟṟiya ceytikaḷik koccippaṭutti eḻutuvatiyē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭōr cilar, 'kaṇṭam aḷaviṟkup periya nilapparappu etuvum ekkālattilum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyatilli' eṉṟu iṟṟi aṟiviyal niṟuviyuḷḷatit talikkīṭākak koṇṭu, kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu paṟṟi nūl eḻutiyōr aṉivariyum tākki ēḷaṉam ceyvatic cuṭṭik kāṭṭum āciriyar, ki.mu. 8000ai oṭṭiya paṉiyūḻi iṟutiyil kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu ēṟattāḻa irunūṟu munnūṟu kal aḷaviṟkuk karinilappakuti (kaṇṭattiṭṭu - Continental Shelf) kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiviṭṭatu paṟṟiyum, akkaṭaṟkōḷukku muṉpu kaṇṭat tiṭṭup pakuti vaḻiyākat tamiḻiya moḻi pēcunar cila pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē teṉṉintiyāvil iruntu pala pakutikaḷukkum paraviyirukkalām eṉpataṟku aṟiviyalāriṉ iṉṟiya muṭivukaḷ uṭaṉpaṭuvati mēṟpaṭiyār kaṇṭukoḷḷātatu kuṟittu nayamākak kaḻaṟukiṟār;
"uriyāciriyarkaḷ kuṟitta 49 nāṭukaḷum cērntu kaṇṭam aḷaviṟku iruntirukka vēṇṭiyatē illi. muṉpattiyil kūṟiyatu pōlak kaṇṭat tiṭṭup pakuti kaṭaluḷ muḻkiyatiyē kaḻaka nūlkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ ceytikaḷ kūṟuvatākak koṇṭāl iṉṟiya aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippu kaḷukkuk kaḻaka ilakkiyak kaṭalkōḷ ceyti muṟṟilum icivatēyākum" 
eṉṟu teḷivuṟuttukiṟār. itu toṭarpāka nām mēlum cilavaṟṟi eṇṇip pārkkalām.
kaṇṭam eṉṟavuṭaṉ āciyā, āppirikkā mutaliyaṉa pōṉṟa tākavē eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. āṉāl intiyā iṉṟu tīvakkuṟi ( tīpakaṟpam ) eṉṟum tuṇik kaṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭālum paratakaṇṭam (parata kaṇṭē ) eṉṟum nāvalan tīvu (camput tīvu ) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum muntiya vaḻakku nōkkat takkatu. avvāṟē nāṭu eṉpatiyum cīṉā, intiyā pōṉṟatākak karuta vēṇṭiyatilli. tamiḻnāṭṭiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭālum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ eṉṟum, toṇṭi nāṭu, koṅkunāṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟuvaṉa uḷa. olliyūr nāṭu, kāṭṭu nāṭu, kōṉāṭu, paṟampu nāṭu, peṇṇi nāṭu, mukkāvaṉāṭu, vēṅkaṭanāṭu eṉpaṉa puṟanāṉūṟu cuṭṭum nāṭukaḷuṭ cila.
ēṟattāḻac ceṉṉi mānakara aḷavē koṇṭa ciṅkappūr iṉṟum oru taṉināṭāka uḷḷatu; nakara nāṭu (City State) eṉappaṭukiṟatu. vāttikaṉum ( Vatican City ) attakiyatē. āttirēliyā iyaṟki yamippāl oru kaṇṭam eṉṟālum aracāṭci niliyip poṟuttu atu oru nāṭēyām.
paṇṭu kumariyakattu ēḻteṅka nāṭu ēḻmaturi nāṭu mutaliyaṉavāka ēḻēḻ nāṭukaḷ eṉappaṭuvaṉavē nāṟpattu oṉpatu nāṭukaḷāka uṟaḻntu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōr ēḻnāṭu eṉpatu ēḻu kōṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa oru maṇṭilam allatu ēḻu vaṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa oru māvaṭṭam pōlvatu eṉalām.
kaṭaṟkōḷāl kumarināṭṭi iḻantamiyiṉ "aṅṅaṉamākiya nilakkuṟikkuc cōḻanāṭṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum, cēramāṉ nāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamum eṉṉum ivaṟṟi iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ" eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟṟum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
ivvāṟṟāṉ aṟiviyalār (kaṇṭattiṭṭu) eṉṟum kaṭal kōṭpaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟum kumarinilap parappik kumarināṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu -- kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṟē kūṟuvatu kūṭa -- aṉṟum iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum nūl vaḻakkiṟkum evvāṟṟāṉum ovvātataṉṟu eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum.
iṉip 'paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟoṭu' eṉat toṭaṅkum cilappatikārap pakutiyāl pāṇṭiyaṉ vaṭaticikkaṇ kaṅkiyāṟṟiyum paṉimaliyiyum taṉatu āṭcikkuriyaṉavākak koṇṭatu. teṉṟicikkaṇ kaṭalkōḷāl tāṉ iḻanta paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟukkum kumarimalikkum īṭākavē eṉpatu teḷiyap paṭutalāl, teṉpāl kumari nilattiṉ nīṭciyāṉatu vaṭapāl pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkum paṉimalikkum iṭippaṭṭa toliviṉ aḷaviṉatu (ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal) eṉa ūkikkalām.
āṉāl iṟṟi niliyil aṟiviyal tuṟikaḷiṉ koḷḷa iṭantarāmiyāl cilappatikāram kūṟiyāṅkup pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉ iḻanta teṉṟiciyāṟṟukkum malikkum īṭāka vaṭaticiyāṟṟiyum maliyiyuṅ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, avviraṇṭun tavirnta nāṭṭukku īṭāka 'malitiriyūrntu eṉat toṭaṅkum kalittokit taravum mēṟkaṇṭa aṭiyārkku nallār uriyum kūṟiyāṅkuc cēra cōḻa nāṭukaḷiṉ ōrōr pakutiyik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ aḷaviṉatē eṉṟum iyipuṟak koḷḷutal cālum.
āyiṉum, ñāla mutaṉmoḻikku aṇukkamāṉatu eṉappaṭum tamiḻukkum, kaṭanta 10000 āṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ piṟa pakuti makkaḷōṭu yātoru toṭarpum iṉṟiyirunta āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpukaḷiyum ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ, mēṟpaṭi ūkam muṟṟilum poruḷaṟṟatu eṉṟu puṟantaḷḷiviṭa muṭiyātu. itu niṟka!
cintuveḷi nākarikam tamiḻaratē eṉpatu ñālamutaṉmoḻi paṟṟiya moḻinūl vallunarkaḷiṉ palvēṟu āyvukaḷ mutal, aṇmiyil mayilāṭutuṟiyi aṭutta cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa putiya kaṟkālak karuviyākiya kaṟkōṭariyil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷ īṟāka uḷḷa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ mūlam niṟuvap peṟṟut tamiḻiṉ toṉmi mēlum valiyuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tol tirāviṭam (Proto Dravidian ) eṉpatu paḻantamiḻē yaṉṟic cilar kūṟuvatu pōl vēṟaṉṟu eṉṉum pāvāṇar karuttu vaḻi niṉṟu "innūlil tol tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉavellām uṇmiyil paḻantamiḻiyē kuṟippatākak koḷka'' eṉṟu āciriyar teḷivuṟuttiyiruppatu naṉṟu.
ippoḻutu palar koṇṭuḷḷa (tavaṟāṉa) karutukōḷiṉpaṭi tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar tāyakam vaṭakiḻakku īrāṉ pakuti eṉṟum aṅkiruntu avarkaḷ intiyā teṉṉāṭu ilaṅki eṉṟu ki.mu. 3000 aḷavil teṟku nōkki iṭam peyarntu ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟum kūṟum 'tirāviṭar iṟakkak koḷkikku nērmāṟākat toltirāṭa moḻi pēcunar vaṭakku nōkkip paraviṉar eṉṉum 'tirāviṭar ēṟṟak koḷkikku ñāṉappirakācar - tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu' eṉac ciṟappup peyar cūṭṭiyiruppatu peritum naṉṟu.
kumarikkaṇṭam paṟṟiyum, cintuveḷi nākarikam paṟṟiyum takka mēṟkōḷkaḷuṭaṉ irāmanātaṉ kūṟum ceytikaḷ ellām avartam muntiya nūlkaḷilum virivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruppaṉavēyāyiṉum cem moḻi eṉṉum niliyil tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum ciṟappiyum valivuṟa eṭutturikkum vakiyil innūlilum iṭam peṟṟiruppatu takkatē.
intiyāvil uḷḷa tamiḻiya, intō āriya, muṇṭā moḻi kaḷiṭiyē potumik kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, inti mutaliya vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ intō āriya moḻikku uriyaṉavalla eṉṟum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum ammāṟupāṭṭut taṉmikaḷ peritum intō āriyam allāta moḻikaḷic cārntavi eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil ( irukku vētam uṭpaṭak) kaṇicamāṉa aḷavukkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷum tamiḻiyak kūṟukaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kūṟum vayavar villiyam cōṉcu, kālṭuvel, parō, emaṉō ākiya moḻiyiyal aṟiñarkaḷiṉ āyvurikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭit tamiḻiṉ ilakkaṇa amitiyum tamiḻc coṟkaḷum camaṟkirutam uḷḷiṭṭa intiya moḻikaḷ aṉittilum paraviyuḷḷaṉa eṉpati viḷakkiyiruppatōṭu, pāvāṇariṉ āyvurikaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi aruḷi, aracēntiraṉ āyvurikaḷum avaṟṟip peritum valiyuṟuttuvati eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tamiḻiṉ etirkālam paṟṟit tolinōkkākak kūrnturikkum nūliṉ iṟutippakuti tamiḻuṇarvāḷar ovvoruvarum - kuṟippāka iḷiya talimuṟiyiṉar uṉṉippākak kavaṉattiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatākum. 
ulaka aḷavil kirēkkam, ilattīṉam mutaliya ēṉic cemmoḻikaḷ pōlātu toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟukāṟum perumpālum citiviṉṟi vaḻaṅki varum cemmoḻiyākiya tamiḻi aric camaṟkirutak kalavi moḻiyākka 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavilēyē toṭaṅkappaṭṭa naccu muyaṟci veṟṟi mukaṭṭi eṭṭuñ cūḻalil nilimi talikīḻāka māṟiviṭṭa tēṉum, kālṭuvel ūṉṟiya vittu maṉōṉmaṇīyam cuntaraṉār, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār mutaliya aṟiñar perumakkaḷ āṟṟiya arumpaṇi kaḷālum araciyal kumukāya iyakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ eḻucciyālum tamiḻp pātukāppum vaḷarcciyum ālamaramāka urukkoṇṭaṉavēṉum payiṟṟu moḻi niliyilum āṭci moḻi niliyilum tamiḻukku eṉṟō ceytirukka vēṇṭiya paṇikaḷ iṉṟuvari oḻuṅkākac ceyyap peṟāmi yāl toyvu nili uḷḷatu eṉpatu naṉku teḷivuṟuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
ulakamayam - paṉṉāṭṭu vaṇikam eṉṉum ceyaṟpāṭṭiṉāl ulakil āṅkilat tavirap piṟamoḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa pēriṭarik kūṟumiṭattup palvēṟu nāṭṭavarkkum nērntuḷḷa pattu vakiyāṉa māperuṅkēṭupāṭukaḷ, mapuveṉ, kirenōpiḷ pōlum moḻiyiyal aṟiñarkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu viṇṭurikkappaṭuḷḷaṉa.
intiyāvil piṟamoḻikku illāmal tamiḻukku maṭṭum uḷḷa uṭpakikaḷākat tēviyiṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalappatum kocci vaḻakku iḻivaḻakkukaḷi eḻuttu naṭiyil pukuttuvatum ākiya 
tamiḻppakivar ceyal ; eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṉṟa peyaril tamiḻukkuḷḷa ciṟanta varivaṭivattic citittut tamiḻi aḻikkat tuṭikkum muḻumakkaḷ ceyal; tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil kivittu mūlap pāṭattic citittum mūlanūl amippi māṟṟiyum veḷiyiṭum naccup pōkku; ākiya mupperun tīṅkukaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu.
ulakamayac curaṇṭaliṉ oru kūṟākavē poruḷiyal paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟiyum cērttu moḻikaḷum āṅkilattāl aḻikkappaṭuvatāl moḻiyaḷavil maṭṭum muyaṟcikaḷi mēṟkoḷvatu niḻaluṭaṉ pōrāṭuvatē eṉṟum, marapuvaḻip poruḷiyali vaḷarppatum ulakamayamākkaliṉ palvēṟu nācakārak kūṟukaḷi etirppatumē uṇmiyāṉa kaḻuvāy eṉṟum, aracukaḷum kaṭcikaḷum mēli nāṭṭup peru mutalāḷiyakkuḻumaṅkaḷukku aṭimiyākiviṭṭa niliyil avaṟṟi nampip payaṉilliyātalāl makkaḷ viḻippuṇarvutāṉ vaḻi eṉṟum, ovvoru tēyattavarum tattam moḻiyiṉ pātukāppukkup pōrāṭa vēṇṭiya nili iruppatāl tamiḻp pātukāppāḷarkaḷ aṇṭi ayal mānilattavaroṭum kalantu ceyaṟpaṭṭu ulaka mayamākkattālum āṅkilattālum intiya moḻikaḷ aṉittum aḻivukku uḷḷāvatit tavirkka muyala vēṇṭum eṉṟum karuttukaḷ teḷivuṟa eṭutturikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟṟāṉ nūliṉ iṟuvāy oru māperum makkaḷ pōrāṭṭattiṟku muṉmoḻivatāka amintuḷḷatu.
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāviyoṭṭi avartam nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum maṟupatippāka orucēra veḷiyiṭṭa tōṭu, taṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭit tokuttup pala tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭup parappiya perumikku uriyavarum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷarumāṉa tamiḻmoḻik kāvalar kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ avarkaḷ pāvāṇar nūl veḷiyīṭṭit toṭarntu pāvāṇar karuttukaḷukku araṇ ceyyum vakiyilāṉa appātturiyāriṉ nūlkaḷ, na.ci.kantiyāviṉ nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭu aruntamiḻp paṇiyit toyviṉṟit toṭarntu varuvatu peritum pārāṭṭi makiḻat takkatu.
tamiḻmaṇ patippaka veḷiyīṭukaḷāṉa pi. irāmanātaṉ nūlvariciyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa innūl tamiḻiṉ palvakic ciṟappu kaḷiyum eṭutturittu valiyuṟuttuvatōṭu avaṟṟik kuṟittup paṉṉāṭṭu moḻi yiyal vallunarkaḷum piṟaruṅ kūṟiyuḷḷa karuttumaṇi kaḷit tāṅkiyuḷḷa peṭṭakamākavum tamiḻp pātukāppuk kēṭaya mākavum amintuḷḷatu.
- iṟikkuruvaṉār

muṉṉuri
cemmoḻit tamiḻ cārpāṉa ceyti kaḷik koṇṭu innūl veḷivarukiṟatu. vaḻakkam pōla itaṉiyum eḻuta eṉṉi ūkkuvitta tamiḻmaṇ patippaka iḷavaḻakaṉārukku eṉatu naṉṟi.
tiṟamāṉa pulamiyum, nuṇmāṇuḻi pulamum, matukiyum uṭiya tamiḻaṟiñar iṟikkuruvaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa aṇinturikkum naṉṟiyuṭi yēṉ. avar eṉṉip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷavaṟṟi eṉpāl uḷḷa aṉpālum eṉṉi ūkkuvikkavum kūṟiya tākak karutukiṟēṉ. aṇinturikkāka nūl muḻu vatiyum paṭittuḷḷatuṭaṉ āṅkāṅku tēviyāṉa tiruttaṅkaḷic ceytu eṉatu naṭiyic ceppaṉiṭṭuḷḷār. ataṟkum naṉṟi eḷitil orē iṭattil kiṭṭātaṉavum, iṉṟiya paltuṟi aṟiviyaṟpula vallunarkaḷ maṟukkoṇātaviyumāṉa ceytikaḷ cilapala vaṟṟi ōriṭattil tokuttup palariṉ pārvikkum koṇṭu varuvatē innūliṉ nōkkamākum.
pi. irāmanātaṉ

patippuri
kālṭuvel 1856 il tamatu "teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇatti veḷiyiṭṭatumē ulaka moḻiyiyalaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ toṉmi, muṉmi, cemmi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, ñāla mutaṉmoḻikkut tamiḻ mika neruṅkiyatāka niṟuvappaṭa vāyppu iruppatiyum uṇarntaṉar. eṉiṉum "cemmoḻi' eṉa oru moḻiyi ēṟṟiṭat toṉmoḻiyāyiṉum ammoḻi ilakkiyam ciṟantatākavum, taṉittaṉmi vāyntatākavum, piṟamoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷi aṭiyoṟṟi uruvākātatumākavum irukka vēṇṭum. 1850 kkup piṉṉartāṉ tolkāppiyam mutalāṉa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭap paṭṭaṉa. 1900 aḷavilēyē moḻi aṭippaṭiyilum cari, ilakkiya aṭippaṭiyilum cari, tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē eṉpati ulaka aṟiñarulakam ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu. intiya naṭuvaṇ aracu aḷavil, tamiḻmoḻi cemmoḻi eṉa mikak kālam tāḻntu 2004 il tāṉ ēṟkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkup palarum kāraṇamāvar - tamiḻarkaḷākiya nām uṭpaṭa. cemmoḻik kōrikki varalāṟṟi innūl iyal iraṇṭil kāṇka.
kālap paḻamiyāl viram pāyntatu! 
nākarikattiṉ nāṟṟaṅ kālatu 
emmoḻikkum atu īṭiṇiyaṟṟa 
cemmoḻi ulakac cintaṉikkellām
ūṟṟāyt tulaṅkum uṇmiyiṉ piñcuṉi! 
eṉṟār pāvēntar. pāvēntariṉ kaṉavi naṉavākki tamiḻic cemmoḻiyāka naṭuvaṇ aracu ēṟṟu aṟivikkac ceyta māṇpumiku tamiḻaka mutalvar kaliñar avarkaḷukkut tamiḻar aṉivarum naṉṟikkaṭaṉ paṭṭavarkaḷ.
cemmoḻit tamiḻttiṭṭattil ēṟkeṉavē toṭaṅki 2005 iliruntu ūkkattuṭaṉ naṭattivarum paṇikaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷi innūliṉ palvēṟu iyalkaḷil kāṇalām. cemmoḻit tamiḻppaṇikaḷi, naṭuvaṇ, mānila aracukaḷiṉ tiṭṭam eṉṟu karutāmal kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷatu paṅkaḷippiyum ātaraviyum tara vēṇṭum.
paṭippaṭiyāka cemmoḻit tamiḻt tiṭṭattiṉ paṇikaḷ virivākkappaṭṭu mēlum pala paṇikaḷ toṭaṅkum eṉpatil aiyamilli. avvāṟu toṭaṅku muṉṉar palatuṟi aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu cemmoḻit tamiḻt tiṭṭa ciṟappu naṭuvamum naṭuvaṇ, mānila aracukaḷum piṉvarum eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum karutiṭa vēṇṭum.
1. ñāla mutaṉmoḻi āyvukaḷukkut tamiḻē vaḻikāṭṭa vallatu eṉpati iyal 4 viḷakkukiṟatu. ittuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻaka moḻiyiyal āyvu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum taṉippaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷum toṭarpu koḷvati ūkkuvikka vēṇṭum.
2. tamiḻiciyē ulaka ici vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭi eṉṟu 'karuṇāmirta cākaram' eṉṟa taṉatu ārāycci nūlil uṟutippaṭutti yuḷḷār aṟiñar āpirakām paṇṭitar. iṉṟu tamiḻici piṟanta nāṭṭilēyē urimiyiḻantu nalintuviṭṭatu. ici nūlkaḷ ellām moḻimāṟṟam peṟṟu vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻici maṟumalarcci peṟa vēṇṭum. tamiḻici nūlkaḷit tamiḻākkam ceytu tamiḻōṭu cērkka vēṇṭum.
3. intiyāvilēyē tamiḻ nāṭṭiltāṉ neṭituyarnta kōpuraṅ kaḷōṭu kūṭiya kōvilkaḷ mikuti. ivi tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaṭak kalikkum ciṟpak kalikkum nēraṭiyāṉa cāṉṟu, eṉṟālum - kōyil kaṭṭaṭakkali maṟṟum ciṟpak kali paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻil oṉṟu kūṭa illi. attaṉip paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷum vaṭa moḻikkuc ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟit tamiḻil moḻipeyarttu tamiḻvaḷam perukka vēṇṭum.
4. tamiḻ maruttuvam - tamiḻukkuriya attuṇit toṉmic ciṟappukaḷiyum koṇṭatu. paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ aṟivukkuc cāṉṟāka amivatu. piṟkālattil cittarkaḷāl vaḷarkkappaṭṭu 'citta maruttuvam eṉṟāṉatu. tamiḻ maruttuvattik koṇṭu vaḷarntavitāṉ intiyāvil luḷḷa maṟṟa mūliki maruttuva muṟikaḷ. iṉṟu tamiḻ maruttuvam ōraḷavu maṟumalarcci peṟṟu varukiṟatu. tamiḻ maruttuvam virivāka ārāyappaṭṭu - paḻantamiḻ maruttuvac cuvaṭikaḷilum - nūlkaḷilumiruntu tamiḻ maruttuvak kalik kaḷañciyam' uruvākkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
5. eṇṇum - eḻuttum vāḻum uyirkaḷukkuk kaṇkaḷ eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. mutaṉ mutal eṇkaḷi uruvākkip payaṉpaṭuttiyavarkaḷ paḻantamiḻarkaḷē. iṉṟiya aṟiviyal vaḷarccikkum - 'kaṇiṉik kalic ceḻippukkum avveṇkaḷē aṭippaṭi. paṇṭit tamiḻakattōṭu toṭarpuṭiya arapu nāṭṭu vaṇikar mūlam avveṇkaḷ ulakeṅkum paravi - iṉṟu arapu eṇ' eṉṟa peyaril vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. eṇ kaṇitamuṟi ārāyappaṭṭu - itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṇṭupiṭippu eṉpati ulakaṟiyac ceyya vēṇṭum.
6. intiya aracu 'cemmoḻikaḷukku vaḻaṅkum attaṉic ciṟappukaḷiyum naṉmikaḷiyum kālantāḻttātu cemmoḻit tamiḻukkum urittākka vēṇṭum. piṉvarum ceyalpāṭukaḷum itil aṭaṅkum:
a) piṟa mānilaṅkaḷilum ayalnāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷil tamiḻ irukkikaḷ niṟuviṭa niti utavi tarutal
ā cemmoḻit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷ veḷivara niti utavit tiṭṭam (tamiḻiliruntu piṟa moḻikaḷilum, piṟamoḻikaḷil liruntu tamiḻilum)
i) tamiḻc cemmoḻi cārnta ciṟanta karuvi nūlkaḷi mūlamākavō, moḻipeyarppākavō veḷiyiṭum aṟiñarkaḷukkum niṟuvaṉaṅ kaḷukkum niti utavit tiṭṭam.
ī) vaḷarntuvarum iḷiya talimuṟi cemmoḻit tamiḻ marapiyum, paṇpāṭṭiyum aṟivataṟku utavum vakiyil, at tamiḻ marapucār veḷiyīṭukaḷi eḷiya naṭiyil vaṇṇappaṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷik koṇṭuvara niti utavit tiṭṭam. caṅkat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟukkum cempatippukaḷ (Critical Editions) veḷivarat takka ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum.
u) ayal nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷākiya kamil cuvalapil, jārj el. hārṭ, ār. ī. āṣar, kurō, cāṉpōrṭu sṭīvar, harālṭu ṣipmāṉ, sṭepāṉ leviṭ, īvā vil taṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ payaṉpeṟum vakiyil nam nāṭṭu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil '' varukip pērāciriyar " ( Visiting Professorship ) paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ ; intiyāviṟku vantu pōvataṟkāṉa payaṇak kaṭṭaṇa utavit tokikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi ēṟpaṭuttutal.
7. (a) ilakkiyap paḻamikkum, aṟiviyal putumikkum ēṟṟa colvaḷam koṇṭa moḻi tamiḻ. aṟiviyal cārnta toḻiṟ kalviyit tamiḻāl kaṟpikka muṭiyum. ulakaḷavil virintu vaḷarum aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ tamiḻākka muṭiyum. tamiḻ - āṅkilam iraṇṭilum valla antantat tuṟi vallunarkaḷ ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭa ūkkuvittal vēṇṭum. ataṟku ēṟṟa vakiyil aṟiviyal kaliccol kaḷañciyamum uruvākkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
(ā) ciṟanta kalikkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ maruttuvam, aṟiviyal, ciṟuvar kalik kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi uruvāka utavi ceytal.
(i) āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarātiyip poṟuttavari 1960kaḷil citamparanātaṉār - appātturiyār tokuttu veḷivanta ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātikkup piṉ ivvakiyil taramāṉa putu muyaṟcikaḷ
illi. takunta aṟiñarkaḷi ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭutti iṉṟiya nilikku ēṟpuṭiya, ulakattaram vāynta āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarāti tokuttu veḷiyiṭappaṭavēṇṭum.
8. (a) cintu veḷiyil akaḻāyvukaḷ naṭattiya takuti vāynta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷa cintuveḷi āyvu muṭivukaḷi mēlum ārāyntu uṇmi niliyi ulakaṟiyac ceyya ēṟkeṉavē cemmoḻit tamiḻ naṭuvam karutiyuḷḷatu ammuyaṟcikaḷit toṭara vēṇṭum.
(ā) tamiḻiṉ toṉmi, tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarccikkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷākiya kipi 600kku muṟpaṭṭa poṟippukaḷ - kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ muḻumiyāṉa āyvu ; tamiḻar nākarikam , vaṇikam mutaliyavaṟṟukku ātāramāṉa tamiḻakak kaṭal, kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷil akaḻvāyvu ; ākiyavaṟṟi muṉippuṭaṉ ceyya vēṇṭum.
(9) tamiḻar perum aḷavil vāḻum ayal nāṭukaḷil tūtarkaḷākat tamiḻarkaḷi amarttuvatu annāṭukaḷil tamiḻ vaḷarccikkup pātukāppu araṇāka amiyum. itu kuṟittut tamiḻaka aracu veḷiyuṟavuttuṟi amiccakattukkup parinturikka vēṇṭum. ayalakat tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē tamiḻ nili peṟuvataṟkāṉa palvaki naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyum eṭukka vēṇṭum.
ulakiṉ ēṉiya cemmoḻikaḷ vāḻntu ceḻitta kālattil em. tamiḻmoḻiyum vāḻntatu. iṉṟu ammoḻikaḷ aḻinta niliyil iḷamikuṉṟā uyirppuṭaṉ uḷḷatu nam tāymoḻiyām teḷḷiya tīntamiḻē...
"ulakiṉ taliciṟantaṉavākak karutappeṟum aṟu cemmoḻikaḷuḷ - uyirppōṭum, ūṭṭattoṭum, cemmāp poṭum, cīroṭum, ciṟappoṭum, tolpaḻantiruvoṭum, kalaṅkaṟāk karuvōṭum, vaḷattōṭum, nalat toṭum, oṭpoṭum, tiṭpoṭum, ulakan tarīiya naṭpōṭum, eṇṇat tiṉikkum iṉiya peṭpōṭum ulavi uvappuṟuttuvatāka, nam teḷḷiya tīntamiḻē iṉṟu - naṉṟu nīṭi niṉṟu nimirnteḻuntu naṭi ciṟantu iyaṅki varukiṉṟamiyi, mutalil nantamiḻar neñcam niṟakka uṇartal vēṇṭum!'' 
eṉṟa collāyvu aṟiñar aruḷiyiṉ neñcak kūṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷ niṉikka vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
tamiḻ moḻi, iṉa nāṭṭuṇarvu eṉ elumpōṭum narampōṭum mūḷiyōṭum, kurutiyōṭum iraṇṭaṟak kalantu ūṟip pōṉatu. atuvē eṉakku uṇavākavum uyirākavum vāḻvākavum āṉatu. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ cemmāppiṉi ulakamelām paṟicāṟṟum vakiyil aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ eḻutiya toṉmic cemmoḻi tamiḻ eṉṉum ivvaruntamiḻ nūliṉi tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukkup putuvaravākak koṭuttuḷḷōm.
innūlukku aṇinturi vaḻaṅkiya pāvāṇar vaḻinili aṟiñar taṉittamiḻk kuricil, teṉ moḻik kuṭumpa marapiṉ mūttavar tirukkuṟaḷ maṇi iṟikkuruvaṉārukku neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi. maṟṟum kaṇiṉi ceytu utaviya mu.na. irāmacuppiramaṇiya rācāvum, "kuṭvil celviyum, pārāṭṭukkuriyar.

vīṟuṭiya cemmoḻi tamiḻmoḻi - ulakam 
vērūṉṟiya nāḷ mutal uyirmoḻi!
vīṟuṭiya cemmoḻi ... 
māṟupaṭum moḻikaḷippōl māṟavilli 
maṅkivarum moḻikaḷippōl maṅkavilli 
vēṟupaṭum moḻikaḷippōl vēṟākavilli 
vīḻntupaṭum moḻikaḷippōl vīḻavilli
vīṟuṭiya cemmoḻi ... 
kūṟupaṭum moḻikaḷippōl kuliyavilli 
koñcip pēcum vaḻakkaṟṟuk kumiyavilli! 
cāṟupaṭṭa maraṅkaḷippōl cāyavilli 
taraṅkeṭṭa maṉitarip pōl tāḻavilli!
vīṟuṭiya cemmoḻi ... 
eṉṉum pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār varikaḷit taṅkaḷ neñca vayalil vitikkiṟēṉ. viḷiviṉ payaṉit tamiḻ ulakam koḷḷaṭṭum.
innūl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya tamiḻkkāppu nūl vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

iyal 1
tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭattiṉ
iṉṟiya ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ
mēli airōppiya nāṭukaḷil paṇṭik kālattilēyē (ippōtiya airōppiya moḻikaḷil ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eviyum tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉṉarē) ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiya kirēkka, ilattīṉ moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷic "cevvilakkiyam " Classical literature eṉa airōppiyar aḻittaṉar; avviru moḻikaḷiyum "cemmoḻikaḷ" Classical languages eṉṟaṉar. Classical eṉpataṉ aṭippaṭip poruḷ "mutal taramāṉa / cevviya / cemmiyāṉa " eṉpatē yākum. avviru moḻikaḷiṉ (ki.mu. 500 - ki.pi. 300 cārnta) ciṟanta paltuṟi ilakkiyaṅkaḷum antanta moḻiyilēyē tāmāka uruvāṉavi; piṟamoḻit tākkattiṉāl alla. avvilakkiyaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷumē piṟṟi airōppiya moḻikaḷukku (āṅkilam, pireñcu, ceru māṉiyam, spāṉiyam, uruciyaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟukku muṉmātiri yāka amintaṉa.
2. piṉṉar 18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyiliruntu ulakiṉ piṟa pakutikaḷil irunta ittakiya moḻikaḷiyum cemmoḻikaḷ eṉa airōppiya moḻiyiyalaṟiñarum ilakkiya aṟiñarum ēṟkalāyiṉar; aḻikkalāyiṉar. cīṉam, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arapu mutaliyavi 1800 toṭakkattil iruntē ivvāṟu karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ toṉmi yāṉa moḻi eṉa 1816 - liruntutāṉ (pirāṉcisviṭ ellis) uṇarappaṭṭatu; 1856-liruntutāṉ (kālṭuvel) ēṟkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl 2000 āṇṭa
'' vari ēṟkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kaḻaka (caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu, tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutaliyavi - acciṭṭu veḷivanta kālam ki.pi. 1870-1900 āṇṭu kaḷēyākum. kirēkka, ilattīṉ, camaṟkirutat tol ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkuc camamāṉa - cila kūṟukaḷil viñciya - paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyam uḷḷati uṇarntatum tamiḻic cemmoḻi eṉa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar 1920 kaḷiliruntē naṭimuṟiyil ottuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. intiya aracu1800 -liruntē camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiyavaṟṟic cemmoḻiyāka "āḷumi naṭimuṟippaṭi" (Executive action) ēṟṟuk koṇṭu avaṟṟi aracu nitiyiliruntu celavu ceytu ūkkuvittu vantatu. tamiḻ paṟṟi attakiya kōrikkikaḷ (tamiḻum cemmoḻi eṉa aṟiñar ēṟṟa) 1900 - liruntu avvappoḻutu eḻuppappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āṉāl tamiḻakap pakutiyiṉ araciyalatikārattik kikkuḷ vittirunta naṭuvaṅkaḷil alla! tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpatik kaṭanta nūṟāṇṭukaḷil viḷakkiya aṟiñar palar karuttukaḷ, akkāla kaṭṭattil cemmoḻiyāka ēṟka viṭutta kōrikkikaḷ paṟṟiya varalāṟṟi iyal 2-il kāṇalām)
3. ceṉṉi mākāṇattil 1920 ticampar mātam nayaṉmik kaṭci āṭcikku vantatu. ceṉṉi mākāṇa aracu 1950 caṉavariyil ceṉṉi mānila aracu āṉatu. 1956 navamparilēyē ceṉṉi mānila aracu 'tamiḻaka aracu' ākiviṭṭatākak karutalām (peyar māṟṟam 1967 -l tāṉ vantatu eṉṟa pōtilum). nayaṉmik kaṭciyilum pērāyak kaṭciyilum mutaṉmi peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ tamiḻarallātār ākiyāl tamiḻic cemmoḻiyāka ēṟka naṭuvaṇaracukku avarkaḷ kōrikki tāṉum vittilar. cāmāṉiyat tamiḻaruḷ perumpāṉmiyiṉar (aṉṟum iṉṟum) tamiḻ, tamiḻar nalaṉippaṟṟik kavalippaṭupavar allar; tannalam, kuṭumpa nalam, cātinalam, kaṭci nalam ivaṟṟukku appāṟpaṭṭavi paṟṟi "eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu emakku" eṉṟu iruppavarkaḷ; eṉavē avarkaḷum tām cārntirunta kaṭcit talivarkaḷiṭam cemmoḻik kōrikkiyi vikkavilli. nallūḻ kāraṇamāka 2004 attōparil tamiḻi naṭuvaṇaracu niliyil "cemmoḻiyāka ēṟpaḷittatu. 2005 cūli mutal tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭatti naṭuvaṇaracu tiṟampaṭac ceyalpaṭutti varukiṟatu. ivvaruñceyalukkuk kāraṇamāṉavar aṉivarum mānila, miya araciṉarum namatu naṉṟikkuriyarāvar.
4. tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭac ceyalpāṭṭup poṟuppi micūriluḷḷa (naṭuvaṇaracu mānta vaḷa amic cakam : uyarkalvit tuṟiyiṉ kīḻ ceyalpaṭum) ''intiya moḻikaḷukkāṉa naṭuvaṇaracu niṟuvaṉattiṉ" amippāṉa ''tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam, atāvatu 
Centre of Excellence for Classical Tamil 
Central Institute of Indian Languages
Manasa gangotri; Mysore 570 006; Karnataka State 
ēṟṟuc cevvaṉē naṭatti varukiṟatu. niṟuvaṉa iyakkunar utaya nārāyaṇaciṅ avarkaḷum tuṇi iyakkunar kē. irāmacāmiyum ivvakiyil pārāṭṭukkuriyavar. virivil inniṟuvaṉam ceṉṉiyiliruntu ceyalpaṭa virukkiṟatu.
5. tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam paltuṟi aṟiñar, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, āyvakaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ kalantāyvu ceytu, piṉvarum ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷi ēṟkeṉavē 2005 cūli mutal toṭaṅki naṭatti varukiṟatu.
(1) tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiya (caṅka ilakkiya) nūlkaḷ 41 - il ovvoṉṟukkum āyvup patippu veḷi yiṭutal - mutalil mūlam ; piṉṉar uriyuṭaṉ Critical Editions of Ancient Tamil Works.
ippaṇiyi 21 tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷik koṇṭu naṭuvam toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu (nāṟpattoru nūlkaḷ paṭṭiyali iyal mūṉṟil kāṇka.)
(2) kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷip piṟa moḻikaḷil peyarttu veḷiyiṭutal. (Translation of Ancient Tamil Works.)
kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟukku ēṟkeṉavē āṅkilattil moḻi peyarppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟukku oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. tirukkuṟaḷukku āṅkila moḻi peyarppukaḷ cumār muppatu uḷḷaṉa; 1794il laṇṭa ṉil veḷiyāṉa Specimens of Hindoo Literature nūlil eṉ. ī. kiṉṭarsli kuṟaḷ mutal 12 atikāraṅ kaḷiliruntu cila kuṟaḷkaḷi eṭuttu āṅkilappaṭutti veḷi yiṭṭār. 1819 il 120 kuṟaḷkaḷukku virivāṉa viḷakkavuriyuṭaṉ ellis tam moḻi peyarppi veḷiyiṭṭār. 1840-1886 kāla aḷavil turu (Drew), ilācaras, pōp pōṉṟa pala muḻumi yāṉa āṅkila moḻipeyarppukkaḷ veḷiyāyiṉa. airōppiya moḻikaḷil mutalil ilattīṉil tāṉ 1730 il vīramāmuṉivar kuṟaḷiṉ mutal irupālkaḷi maṭṭum peyarttu veḷiyiṭṭār. kuṟaḷiṉ ēṉiya airōppiya moḻi ākkaṅkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi pireñcu - ēriyal (1848); cerumaṉ - kirāl (1856 ākum.
ikkuṟaḷ moḻipeyarppukaḷ ulaka aḷavil perum tākkatti ēṟpaṭuttiṉa (kāṇka iyal 3: tirukkuṟaḷ)
kaḻaka ilakkiya akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷ vēṟu em moḻip pāṭalkaḷilum illāta taṉicciṟappuṭiyavi eṉpati ē. kē. irāmāṉucaṉuṭiya kuṟuntokiyiṉ arumiyāṉa āṅkila ākkam 1967 il (The Interior Landscape) veḷivantatum paṉṉāṭṭu ilakkiyavāṇarum teḷḷitiṉ uṇarntaṉar.
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cilavaṟṟukku ēṟkeṉavē uḷḷa (āṅkila moḻipeyarppukaḷi) aṟiñarkaḷik koṇṭu āyvu ceytu avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantavaṟṟi naṭuvam virivil acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭavirukkiṟatu. aṭutta kaṭṭamākap putiya āṅkila moḻi peyarppukaḷum ceyyappaṭum. putiya moḻipeyarppukkāka ovvoru nūlukkum tamiḻ aṟiñar oruvar, moḻi peyarppāḷar oruvar, āṅkila aṟiñar oruvar ākiyōrik koṇṭa mūvar kuḻuvi naṭuvam amikkum.
piṟamoḻikaḷil : inti, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, pireñcu mutaliya moḻikaḷilum kaḻaka ilakkiyap peyarppukaḷi naṭuvam virivil koṇaravirukkiṟatu.
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷ, kuṟippāka vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattilum kāṇaviyalātaṉavaṟṟip paṟṟiya aṟiñar kūṟṟukkaḷi iyal 3il kāṇalām. kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷip piṟa moḻikaḷil peyarppatu paṟṟiyatu iyal 4.
(3) varalāṟṟumuṟit tamiḻilakkaṇam veḷiyiṭutal Historical Grammar of Tamil
tamiḻ oliyaṉiyal, oliyiyal, peyarccolliyal, viṉic colliyal, iṭiccoṟkaḷ, toṭarpaṉiyal (Phonetics, Phonology, Noun morphology, Verb morphology, particles. Syntax) ākiya āṟu tuṟikaḷilum moḻiyilaṟiñar aṟuvarik koṇṭu āḷukkoru iyali eḻutuvittu, tokuttu ivvilakkaṇa nūli naṭuvam veḷiyiṭa virukkiṟatu. kāla varampukaḷ, cemmoḻikkālam kimu. 300 - ki.pi. 600; iṭikkālam ki.pi. 601-1600; ikkālam ki.pi. 1601 mutal - eṉṟavāṟu amiyum.
(4) tamiḻiṉ toṉmi : piṟa aṟivuppulaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇinta āyvu : Antiquity of Tamil: an interdisciplinary Approach.
tamiḻ iciyiyal, kaḻakakkālam cārnta kukik kal veṭṭukaḷ, kaḻakak kālap pāṉi ōṭukaḷ, kācukaḷ, muttiri kaḷ, naṭukaṟkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil uḷḷa poṟippukaḷ kalveṭṭu kaḷ ivi cārnta āyvukaḷi naṭuvam ūkkuvikkum cintu veḷi nākarika eḻuttāyvukaḷiyum ūkkuvikkum. putiya akaḻvāyvukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷavum vaḻivakikaḷ kāṇap paṭum.
paḻantamiḻ icippāṭalkaḷi icikkum (90 maṇit tuḷi) kuṟuntakaṭu oṉṟu aṇiyamākiyuḷḷatu.
iṉṟiya nāstirātik / yūrēciyāṭik āyvāḷarkaḷ, ñāla mutaṉ moḻi āyvāḷarkaḷ, tolliyalāḷar, marapaṇu āyvāḷar ākiyōr pārviyil tamiḻiṉ toṉmi paṟṟi iṉṟiya aṟiñarulakam koṇṭuḷḷa karuttukkaḷi iyal 4il kāṇalām.
(5) tamiḻiṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷ : (ka) kālantōṟum iruntavi (ilakkiya, kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷiṉ paṭi ) maṟṟum (u) iṉṟu tamiḻakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum uḷḷavi : Diachronic and synchronic study of Tamil dialects.
ipporuḷ cārnta 1100 nūlkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷi naṭuvam tokuttuḷḷatu. vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc col akarāti vaṭṭāra vaḻakku nilappaṭam (Dialect Atlas) ākiyavaṟṟi uruvākkuvatu nōkkamākum. pala aṭippaṭikaḷil ituvarit tokutta 40,000 vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ (palvēṟu toḻil kaḷukkuriya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ, kumukap pirivukaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷ uṭpaṭa) kaṇiṉiyil ēṟṟappaṭuḷḷaṉa; paṇi toṭarkiṟatu.
(6) intiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭiyē potumi India as a Linguistic Area
itu paṟṟiya āyvukaḷukku utavum vakiyil tamiḻ - piṟamoḻi akarātikaḷ, piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi veḷiyiṭappaṭum. paṇṭārakarkaḷ tāmas parō - marrē pārṉcaṉ emeṉō tokutta tirāviṭac coṟpiyal akaramutali Dravidian Etymological Dictionary (1961 / tiruttiya patippu 1984kku iṇipput tokuti oṉṟi naṭuvam uruvākki varukiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa intō - āriya moḻik kuṭumpam, muṇṭā moḻikkuṭumpam pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ moḻi kaḷil uḷḷa tamiḻiyak kūṟukaḷ paṟṟi ituvari moḻiyiyal 
aṟiñar kaṇṭuḷḷa muṭivukaḷiṉ curukkatti iyal 5 il kāṇalām.
(7) cemmoḻit tamiḻ āyvu kuṟitta kaṇiṉi nūlakam uruvākkutal Digital library for ancient Tamil Studies:
ivvāyvu cārnta 3800 nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya viḷattaṅkaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttilum urōmaṉ eḻuttilum tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyvuc ceytikaḷit tamiḻilō āṅkilattilō uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ peṟakkūṭiya viḷattak kaḷañciyamum Tamil - English Bilngual Thesurus uruvāki varukiṟatu. tēviyāṉa meṉ poruḷ mutaliyaṉavum aṇiyamākiṉṟaṉa.
(8) cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyatti "āṉliṉ" vaḻik kaṟpittal . Online Teaching of Classical Tamil
akappāṭal, puṟappāṭal, ilakkiyam, aṟanūl, ilakkaṇam (ceyyuḷiyal uṭpaṭa) ākiya aintu vakic kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum mottam 300 maṇinēram ''āṉliṉ' vaḻi kaṟpikkat tiṭṭamiṭṭu, paṇi toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.
(9) cemmoḻit tamiḻilakkiyat tokuppik kaṇiṉiyil uruvākkutal. Corpus development for Classical Tamil works.
cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ 41 iṉ āyvup patippukaḷ veḷiyāṉatum ippaṇi toṭaṅki niṟivēṟum ; ataṟkāṉa tiṭṭam uruvākiviṭṭatu.
(10) cemmoḻit tamiḻ cār paṭakkāṭcikaḷi uruvākkutal. Visual episodes on Classical Tamil
tamiḻ moḻi, kaḻaka ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam, kalveṭṭiyal, kāciyal, kali, kaṭṭumāṉam, camayam, tamiḻ ayalnāṭukaḷil paraval pōṉṟa poruṇmikaḷil ēṟattāḻa 100 paṭakkāṭcikaḷ uruvākkappaṭum. mutaṟkaṇ 13 paṭakkāṭcikaḷ terinteṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyam - 4 tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ - 2 ; tamiḻarkāciyal - 2; tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tolvaravu - 2; kaḻakak kālat tuṟimukaṅkaḷ / talinakaraṅkaḷ / vaṇika naṭuvaṅkaḷ - 3
6. tamiḻ nāṭṭil palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam kaṭanta ōrāṇṭukku mēlākap pala karuttaraṅkukaḷiyum āyvaraṅ kaṅkaḷiyum naṭatti varukiṟatu. annaṭuvattuṭaṉ iṇintu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷum, tamiḻppaṟṟāḷarum, tamiḻ ilakkiyam, moḻi, paṇpāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ mēmpāṭṭukkum potu makkaḷiṭiyē ivi paṟṟiya ārvatti ūṭṭuvataṟkum muyalvārkaḷāka
7. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṉ aṉittup poruṇmikaḷiyum kuṟittu naṭuvaṇ aracukku aṟivuri taruvataṟku aṟiñar muṉivar vā.ce.kuḻanticāmi talimiyil vallunar patiṉmarik koṇṭa 'tamiḻmoḻivaḷarcci āyam Tamil language Promotion Board” oṉṟi naṭuvaṇ aracu amittuḷḷatu. tamiḻc cemmoḻi aṟiñarkaḷuḷ ciṟantavarkaḷukkum ayal nāṭṭavarkkum, nam nāṭṭavarkkum āṇṭutōṟum cila ciṟappu virutukaḷ vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭamum aṟivikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻc cemmoḻi aṟiñarkaḷil ciṟantavarkaḷukku āṇṭu tōṟum matippuc cāṉṟitaḻum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. iḷam akavi aṟiñarkaḷi ūkkuvikkap paricut tokikaḷum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. muṉivar paṭṭattukku mēl niliyilāṉa āyvup paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ aintu muṉivar paṭṭa āyvup paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ pattu ākiyavaṟṟiyum āṇṭutōṟum vaḻaṅkuvatāka naṭuvaṇ aracu maṉitavaḷa amiccakam 14 - 7 - 2005 aṉṟu aṟivittuḷḷatu. virivil cemmoḻit tamiḻ naṭuvam ceṉṉiyiliruntu ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟkāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷi mānila aracum miya aracum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉa.

iyal 2
''tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē': karutturikaḷ, kōrikkikaḷ paṟṟiya
oru varalāṟṟup pārvi
1. tamiḻ taṉimoḻi, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ (teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, maliyāḷam mutaliyavi) tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattic cārntavi eṉpati mutalil 1816 il niṟuviyavar pirāṉcis viṭ ellis . piṉṉar 1828 il pi.ji. pāpiṅṭaṉum 1853 il ār. hoyciṅṭaṉum ikkaruttik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 1856 il kālṭuvel tamatu "tirāviṭa allatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam" nūli veḷiyiṭṭu tamiḻ intō airōppiya moḻikaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatākalām eṉṟum ñālamutaṉ moḻikku mika neruṅkiyatāka irukkalām eṉṟum niṟuviṉār. varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyalil annūl ulaka aḷavil mikac cīriya iṭattip peṟṟiruppatākum. (ēṟkaṉavē coṉṉapaṭi) 1870-1900 kāla aḷavil caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ aṉittum accil veḷi vantatum paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyam cevvilakkiyam eṉpatiyum tamiḻmoḻi cemmoḻi eṉpatiyum naṭimuṟiyil paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarum ēṟpārāyiṉar (naṭuvaṇaraci ēṟkac ceyya 2005 vari āṉataṟkut tamiḻariṉ ūkkak kuṟivē kāraṇam eṉpatiyum kaṇṭōm. kaṭanta nūṟu āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉap pakarnta aṟiñar cilar kūṟṟukkaḷi ivviyalil kāṇpōm:
2. palkalikkaḻaka vakuppukaḷil intiyāveṅkum cemmoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutam maṭṭum kaṟṟuttantāl pōtum, uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷ Vernaculars kaṟṟut taravēṇṭām eṉa karcāṉ pirapuviṉ naṭuvaṇaracu 1902ai oṭṭik kaṭṭuppāṭu ceyya muyaṉṟa poḻutu ataṟku etirāṉa parappurikaḷi vi.kō.cūriya nārāyaṇa cāttiriyār, em.es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷi , lācaras mutaliyavarkaḷ ceytaṉar.
akkāla kaṭṭattil tamatu tamiḻmoḻi varalāṟu (1903) nūlil tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē eṉa piṉvarumāṟu kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttiṉār cāttiriyār :
''vaṭamoḻi, ilattīṉ, kirīkku mutaliyaṉa pōlat tamiḻ moḻiyum 'uyartaṉic cemmoḻi' yāmāṟu ciṟitu kāṭṭuvāḷ. tāṉ vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṉkaṇuḷḷa pala moḻikaṭkut talimiyum avaṟṟiṉum mikka mēta kavuṭimiyumuḷḷa moḻiyē 'uyarmoḻi. iv vilakkaṇattāṉ ārāyumiṭattut tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kellān talimiyum avaṟṟiṉum mikka mētakavum uṭimiyāl tāṉum uyarmoḻiyē yeṉka. tāṉ vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṟ payilum maṟṟiya moḻikaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟit taṉittu iyaṅkavalla āṟṟal cāṉṟatē 'taṉimoḻi' eṉappaṭum. tāṉ piṟamoḻikaṭkuc ceyyum utavi mikuntum avi taṉakkuc ceyyum utavi kuṟintum iruttalē vaḻakkāṟu. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉutavi kaḷiyappaṭiṉ, teluṅku mutaliyaṉa iyaṅkutalollā; maṟṟut tamiḻ moḻi avaṟṟiṉutaviyillāmalē ciṟitu miṭarppaṭutaliṉṟit taṉittu iṉimiyiṉ iyaṅkavallatu. iḵtu intiya moḻi nūṟpulavarkaḷ palarkkum oppa muṭintatu. ātaliṉ tamiḻ taṉimoḻiyē yeṉka. iṉic cemmoḻiyāvatu yātu? ''tiruntiya paṇpuñ cīrtta nākarikamum, poruntiya tūymoḻi pukal cemmoḻiyām" eṉpatu ilakkaṇam immoḻi nūlilakkaṇam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇṇum amintiruttal tēṟṟam. eṉṉi? iṭarppaṭṭa coṉ muṭipukaḷum poruṇmuṭipukaḷumiṉṟic coṟṟāṉ karutiya poruḷik kēṭṭāṉ teḷḷitiṉuṇara valla tāyp paḻiyaṉa kaḻintu putiyaṉa pukuntu tiruttameyti niṟṟalē tiruntiya paṇpeṉappaṭuvatu. itu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇ muṟṟum amintiruttal kāṇka. nāṭṭiṉ nākarika mutirccik kēṟpac coṟkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭup pāṣikkum nākarika nalam viḷittal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu coṟkaḷēṟpaṭumiṭattup piṟa pāṣic coṟkaḷaṉṟit taṉ coṟkaḷē mikutal vēṇṭum. iviyum uyartaṉittamiḻ moḻikkup poruntuvaṉa vām ākavē tamiḻ tūymoḻiyumām. eṉavē tamiḻ
cemmoḻiyeṉpatu tiṇṇam itupaṟṟi yaṉṟē toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ moḻi 'centamiḻ' eṉa nallicip pulavarāl naviṉṟōtap peṟuvatā yiṟṟu. ākavē teṉṉāṭṭiṉkaṭ ciṟantoḷirāniṉṟa amiḻtiṉumiṉiya tamiḻ moḻi evvāṟṟāṉ ārāynta vaḻiyum 'uyartaṉic cemmoḻi ' yē yām eṉpatu niccayam. ivvaḷavuyarvuñ ciṟappum vāynta arumit tamiḻmoḻiyi uṇṇāṭṭup puṉmoḻikaḷō ṭoruṅ keṇṇutal tavirttu, vaṭanāṭṭuyartaṉic cemmoḻi camaṟkirutameṉak koṇṭāṟ pōlat teṉṉāṭṭuyar taṉic cemmoḻi tamiḻeṉak koṇṭu pukutalē ēṟpuṭittām''
3. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamulian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē kālṭuvel kālam vari vaḻaṅkiya peyari avar tāṉ ''tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccit tiripuvaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil "tamiḷa", taṇṭiyiṉ eḻutiya avanticuntari katiyil 'tramiḷa' eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvāku kuṭimarapiṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum 'tamiḷa' tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉar kumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantiravārttikattil
"tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva'' (appaṭi tirāviṭa ( tamiḻ ) maṟṟum piṟa moḻikaḷil) 
eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ - tamiḷ - tramiḷa - tramiṭa-trāviṭam eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci.parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyi acciṭṭa poḻutu apattamāka
"āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva"
eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉa vācakatti kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō (Linguistic Survey of India: 1906) pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟi piṭi cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri42 ām tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. (kuñña ṇṇirājā Annals of Orental Research tokuti 28 (1979) kaṭṭuriyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla parṉel uṭiya tavaṟāṉa vācakattit tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟit "tamiḻiyum, teluṅkiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār" eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar)
4. civacittānta makācamācam taṉatu 1918 mānāṭṭil ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam tamiḻi oru cemmoḻiyāka aṟivikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīrmāṉam niṟivēṟṟiyatu. karantit tamiḻccaṅkam 25/5/1919 - lēyē ulakac cemmoḻi kaḷiṉ mutaṉ moḻiyeṉak karutaṟkuriya aṉittut takutikaḷiyum tamiḻmoḻi uṭiyatāyiruppatāl ataṉi attakiya moḻiyāka āṭciyāḷar karuti ai.ci.es paṭṭat tiṟku ati oru pāṭamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu oru tīrmāṉamum, tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu, toṉmiyum cīrmiyum cemmiyum vāyntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyeṉap palatiṟattārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭu vatāl, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattār, tām itu nāḷum koṇṭirunta koḷkiyi māṟṟit tamiḻmoḻi ōr uyar taṉic cemmoḻiyē eṉpati oppuk koṇṭu itteṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakattil ataṟku muṟippaṭi mutal iṭamum (First Place) ēṟpum (Recognition) tara vēṇṭum eṉṟu maṟṟoru tīrmāṉamum niṟivēṟṟi iraṇṭu poruṇmi kaḷiyum annāḷiya intiya aracukkum ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattukkum aṉuppiyatu. pulavar cuntararācaṉ vaḻikāṭṭutalil pala āṇṭukaḷāka muṉintu ceyalpaṭum talinakart tamiḻc caṅkam pōṉṟa amippukaḷum piṟavum tamiḻic cemmoḻi eṉa naṭuvaṇaracu aṟivikka vēṇṭumeṉa 2000 -liruntu eṭutta muyaṟcikaḷil karantit tamiḻccaṅkamum iṇintu ceyal paṭṭatu. itu toṭarpāka tilli ceṉṟa cāliṉi iḷantiriyaṉ 2000 - il tilliyil pēruntu mōti uyir tuṟantatu oru tuyara nikaḻvu ākum.
5. 15.03.1951 ām nāḷaṉṟu naṭipeṟṟa intiya ilakkiyak kaḻakattiṉ (cākittiya akāṭami) toṭakka mānāṭṭil annāḷ naṭuvaṇaracuk kalvi amiccar apulkalām ācāt pēcciṉ pakuti varumāṟu: "intiya araciyaṟ caṭṭam (tēciya moḻikaḷāka) ēṟṟuḷḷa patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷil camaṟ kirutamum tamiḻum aṭaṅkum. camaṟkirutam taṉittaṉmi vāyntatu eṉavē ciṟanta cemmoḻiyāka atu ñāyap paṭi ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻum vaḷamāṉa toṉmiyāṉa ilak kiyaṅkaḷip peṟṟuḷḷatu. ataṉ ceyyuḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ayal moḻikaḷil peyarkkappaṭum ciṟappu vāyntavi. tamiḻ uṇmiyākavē ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻi eṉpatiyum cem moḻit takuti paḻaṅkālattilēyē eytiviṭṭatu eṉpatiyum nām niṉiviṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum"
6.1960kkup piṉṉark kaḻaka ilakkiyattil tuṟi pōyavar kaḷum ēṉiya cemmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷi uṇarntavarkaḷum āṉa aṟiñarkaḷ kamil cuvelepil, jārj hārṭ, ē.kē. irāmāṉucaṉ ākiyōr kaḻaka ilakkiyamāṉatu piṟa cem moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum ciṟappu vāynta taṉittaṉmi vāyntatu eṉpati viḷakkiyuḷḷa pakutikaḷ virivāka iyal 3il tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
7. aṇmiyil cemmoḻit tamiḻ paṟṟiyum naṭuvaṇaracu tamiḻic cemmoḻiyāka aṟivikkak kaṭanta kāl nūṟṟāṇṭākap pala iyakkaṅkaḷum piṟarum ceyta muyaṟcikaḷ paṟṟiyum veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavi vā. ce. kuḻanticāmi (2004, 2005) aruḷi (2005) mutaliyaviyākum. micūriluḷḷa tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam veḷiyiṭum kālāṇṭu itaḻ "cemmoḻiyum 1-1&2 (ak. ticampar 2006; caṉavari - mārc 2007) iṉṟu naṭantuvarum cemmoḻit tiṭṭac ceyaṟpāṭukaḷiṉ viḷattaṅkaḷic cevvaṉē tantu varukiṉṟatu.
iyal 2kkāṉa nūṟpaṭṭiyal
Ellis, Francis Whyte (181) "Note to the introduction" in A grammar of the Teloogoo language by A.D. Campbell
Babington, Benjamin Guy (1830)An account of the sculptures and inscriptions at Mahamalipur; Transactions of the Royal Asiatic Society Vol II (Paper read on 12 - 7 - 1828) :
“I cannot touch on the subject of ancient Tamil characters without remarking, that their extreme simplicity seems one among many circumstances, which indicate that the language is of very high antiquity. The Sanscrit of the South of India is written in characters (the Grantha) derived from the Tamil, but they are much more complicated, and therefore probably posterior in point of antiquity. The peculiar structure of the Tamil language, wholly dissimilar from the Sanserit, its deficieney in aspirated consonants, its possession of letters and sounds not found in Sanscrit, its division into dialects, one of which contins but few words of Sanscrit derivation; and sounds not found in Sanscrit, its division into dialects, and lastly, its locality at the southern extremity of India, would seem likewise to indicate an independent origin, and one of at least equal antiquity with the Sanscrit itself; but this is a subject foreign to that now under consideration, and deserving a more, lengthened discussion than the limits of a note will allow."
Hoisington, Rev Henry (1853) Brief Notes on the Tamil language. Journal of the American Oriental Society III; Article ix. (Paper read on 19 - 5 - 1852):
“No language can be more concise, copious, pliant or mellifluous than Shen Tamil”
(Tamil)“is believed to be the radix of the Telugu, the Canarese, the Malayalim, the Tuluva and other dialects, which constitute the speech of some twenty or thirty millions of people. So that it may will be considered as occupying Southern India”
Caldwell, Robert (1856; II Edn 1875) A Comparative grammar of the Dravidian or South Indian family of languages.

Meenakshisundaram, T.P. (1956) Address to Tamil section of All India oriental conference 1955 at Annamalinagar; TAMIL CULTURE V - 2 April 1956 [Reference to Abul Kalam Azad's 15.5.1951 address (“Tamil is really a classical language”]

iyal 3
cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ
(caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ) taṉicciṟappu
muccaṅkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟi mutaṉ mutalil ki.pi. 10m nūṟṟāṇṭil iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷukku uri yeḻutiya (piṟkāla) nakkīrarum, avarukkup piṉṉar cilappati kārattukku uriyeḻutiya aṭiyārkku nallārum, kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar :
tamiḻccaṅkam 		āṇṭa pāṇṭiya 		caṅkap pulavar 	pāṭiya pulavar 	caṅkam 
maṉṉar 			eṇṇikki 	eṇṇikki 	ceyalpaṭṭa

taliccaṅkam		 89				549		4449 		4440 
(teṉmaturi)

(kapāṭapuram)

(maturi = kūṭal )

ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiru kaḻakaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyavaṟṟi varalāṟ ṟāciriyar, varalāṟṟuṇarvu uṭiyavarkaḷ ēṟpatilli .
2. perumpālāṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum piṟa tuṟi aṟiñarkaḷum kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkattukkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum maturiyil tamiḻk kaḻakam - atiyotta ilakkiya pīṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
"maturiyil pāṇṭiya vēntar ātaravil cilakālam tamiḻppulavar amippu (caṅkam) oṉṟu ceyalpaṭṭu vantatu uṇmiyāṉa ceytiyāka irukkalām" - a. nīlakaṇṭa cāstiri.
'	'tamiḻccaṅkam iruntatu eṉṟa marapuc ceyti iṭiviṭātu iruntu varukiṟatu. (intiyāvil ittakiya marapuc ceyti vēṟeṅkum illi.)
tamiḻc cevvilakkiya ('caṅka'') kālattil ilak kiyaṅkaḷi vaḷarttu, taram kaṇṭu ūkkuvitta oru amippu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu" - cēviyar es. taṉināyakam.
''kaḻaka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷil taṉippāṭal kaḷāyiṉum avaṟṟiṭiyē naṭi, coṟṟoki, yāppu, pāṭuporuḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. 'ilakkiya amippu cārnta taṉmi " "academism" eṉa itik kūṟalām. akātemi pōṉṟa ilakkiya amippu illāmal attakiya oṟṟumi ēṟpaṭṭirukkātu. ki.pi. 1ai oṭṭi maturiyil attakiya akātemi, atā vatu kaḻakam iruntatu eṉa muṭivu ceyvataṟkāṉa pala piṭipāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. - kē.vi. cuvelapil Tamil literature (1974) Otto HarrassOvitz. Wiesbaden. 
ikkarutti mēlum araṇ ceyvaṉa maturiyil (kūṭalil) kaḻakam iruntatiyō allatu akkaḻakattiṉ ceyal pāṭṭiyō kuṟikkum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum ākum. avi varumāṟu. 
"tamiḻ keḻu kūṭal'						 puṟam 58:13 
''tolāṇi nal āciriyar 
puṇar kūṭṭu uṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉ 
nilam taru tiruviṉ neṭiyōṉ”
- maturi ka. 761-3 '
'tamiḻ nili peṟṟa tāṅkaru marapiṉ 
makiḻnaṉi marukiṉ maturi 				- ciṟupāṇ. 66 - 7 
"tamiḻ viyi” 						- paripāṭal 6:63 
"tamiḻ vēli" maturiyik kuṟittatu		 - paripāṭal 6:63 
naṉ pāṭṭup pulavaṉāyc caṅkam ēṟi	 	- tirunāvukkaracar 
antaṇ maturit toki ākkiṉāṉum 	- tiruñāṉacampantar 
kūṭaliṉ āynta oṇ tīntamiḻiṉ tuṟivāy 
nuḻintaṉiyō 		- māṇikkavācakar tirukkōviyār 20 
teṉmaturāpuram ceytum aṅkataṉil aruntamiḻ naṟ 
caṅkam irīit tamiḻ vaḷarttum - taḷavāypuram ceppēṭu
(parāntaka vīranārāyaṇaṉ ki.pi. 9m nū) 
"māpāratam tamiḻppaṭuttum maturāpuric caṅkam vittatum 
periya ciṉṉamaṉūrc ceppēṭu (parāntaka vīra 
nārāyaṇaṉ makaṉ irāca cimmaṉ)
''tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal caṅkap palakiyil amarntirunta pulavar parampariyic cārnta eṭṭiccāttaṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippu. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kalveṭṭu (ARE 334/1929-30) erukkaṅkuṭi, cāttūr vaṭṭam.
3. potuvākak kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka ēṟkappaṭum nūlkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ kālavarampum (perumpālāṉa paṉṉāṭṭup paltuṟi aṟiñarkaḷum iṉṟu ēṟpatu) varumāṟu:

iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal 60 nūṟpā 			ki.pi6 ām nū.
cēviyar es taṉināyakam taṉatu 'iyaṟkiyum tamiḻc ceyyuḷum" (1996) eṉṉum nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa piṉvarum karuttu mika iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
"tamiḻc (caṅka) ceyyuḷ tamiḻakattilēyē karukkoṇṭu vaḷarntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa aṉittuc cāṉṟukaḷiyum koṇ ṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ pēcunar veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantu iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟō, varum poḻutē vaḷarcciyaṭinta tamiḻ moḻi, ilakkiyatti uṭaṉ koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟō kūṟuvataṟkuk kaḻaka ilakkiyam iṭam taravilli eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ.''
4. mikap paḻiya kaḻaka nūlkaḷilēyē (tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil toṉmi cāṉṟavi) tamiḻ varivaṭivam, eḻuttumuṟi kuṟittum paṇ paṭṭa ilakkiyam kuṟittum teḷivāka, virivākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ai. makātēvaṉ (2003) karuttu , acōkaṉuṭiya tamiḻ - pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ varivaṭivam uru vākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. ikkaruttu ēṟkattakkatāka illi. kāraṇam mēṟcoṉṉa ki.mu. 300 cārnta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ uruppeṟuvataṟkuc cila pala nūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ varivaṭivamum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. koṟkiyil 1970il akaḻn teṭukkappaṭṭa pāṉi ōṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttiṉ kālam ki.mu. 700 eṉa appoḻutē uṟutiyākkap paṭṭatu. koṭumaṇalil aṇmiyil akaḻnta pāṉi ōṭṭu eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ kālamum ki.mu. 500kku muntiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉa irācaṉ (2004) niṟuviyuḷḷār. āticcanallūril 2004 il naṭanta akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭa pāṉi ōṭṭup poṟippiṉ kālamum karimak kaṇippiṉpaṭi ki.mu. 700ai oṭṭiyatāka amiya lām eṉac ceyti vantuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟiyum piṟa cāṉṟu kaḷiyum cuṭṭi irācaṉ (2004), naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr "tamiḻukkuk ki.mu. 800ai oṭṭiyē oru taṉi vari vaṭivam iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ataṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka tamiḻ - pirāmi varivaṭivam (lipi) ki.mu. 300 il uruvākkap paṭṭirukkum" eṉpataṟkāṉa valuvāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷi muṉ vittuḷḷaṉar. cintuveḷi nākarika varivaṭivam tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatu āṉapaṭiyāl pirāmi varivaṭivamum atiliruntē uruvākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar hīrās (New Review; cūli 1936). tamatu paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ 1938 nūlil ti.nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ kūṟiyatiyum niṉivu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
"pirāmi lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟ paṭavilliyeṉṟum uyir eḻuttukkaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappiyaḷippatum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ varkka eḻuttukkaḷik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣikkeṉa amik kappaṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭum paṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ pirāmi mutalil tamiḻukkeṉa aminta lipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu'' 
vaṭanāṭṭu varalāṟṟaṟiñar es. ār. kōyal (1979) karuttum acōka pirāmi eḻuttu paṭippaṭiyāka (vaṭanāṭṭil) uruvāṉa taṉṟu; atu oru kāla kaṭṭattil (ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 300 eṉak kūṟalām) putitāka uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum.
5. tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta ki.mu. 1900 - ki.mu. 300 cārnta peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ toṭarcciyē kaḻaka ilakkiya kālam; annākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavi kaḻaka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum irācaṉ pōṉṟavar kaḷuṭiya aṭutta muṭivākum. āka ivvārāyccikaḷ mēlum muṉṉēṟum poḻutu tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300kkum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa aṉivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppuḷḷatu.
6. ittaṟuvāyil iṉṉoṉṟiyum kūṟa vēṇṭum. ki.mu. 300kku muṟpaṭṭa kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eviyumē namakkuk kiṭikkavilli . ki.mu. 300 - kipi. 300 kālakaṭṭattic cārnta kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum muḻumiyāka namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. ki.pi. 300ai oṭṭik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷit tokuttavarkaḷ evvevaṟṟi viṭṭu vikkalām eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷō avaṟṟi maṭṭum tokuttu viṭṭu maṟṟavaṟṟi nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. tokuttavaṟṟilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytirukkalām. nuṇṇaṟi vuṭiyavarkaḷ itupaṟṟi 1877-liruntu terivittuḷḷavaṟṟik kāṇpōm : -
cārlas ī kōvar : teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭal kaḷ (The Folk Songs of Southern India) 1871: tiṭṭa miṭṭuc citikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟi yeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅ kālat tamiḻ nūl etiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattik kiviṭṭut toṉma (purāṇakkatikaḷi nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattic citittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷilum annūlkaḷi kayavāḷit taṉamākac citittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūlanūl kaḷiṉ karutti uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. civattampi (1986yuem ikkaruttiṉarē.
hayūnevil : ti tapropēṉiyaṉ (The Taprobanian) cūṉ 1896 "maturit tamiḻk kaḻakamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkap paṭṭapoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷki vāṭi irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattil makāvamcam iruntatupōl pāṇṭi yarkaḷ varalāṟṟik kūṟum oru nūl maturik kaḻaka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka irunta aḻikkappaṭṭu ataṟku māṟṟāka tiruviḷi yāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum. aḻikkap paṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citivukaḷuṭaṉ, caṅkamarkaḷ (pārp paṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ ) eṇṇam, camayam ākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉikatikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇintu ap purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu".
jāṉ spiyars "ñāṉam tōṉṟiya iṭattiliruntu" vālyūs Values II - 10 cūli 1957 : "cintuveḷi nākarika muttirikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (toltamiḻ) vācakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal koṇṭa (aḻintupōṉa) paṇṭi nūlkaḷip paṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum puttar kālamākiya ki.mu. 600kku muntiya nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟukūṭa kiṭik kātataṟ kāṉa kāraṇatti nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. (tol tamiḻaruṭiya) paṇṭiya āvaṇaṅkaḷi āriyarkaḷ oṉṟu viṭāmal aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatē atu.
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉ : tolkāppiyam - collatikāram : kallāṭar uri (1971) : "tolkāppiyatti ataṉ āciriyar enta uruvattil viṭṭuc ceṉṟārō anta vaṭivattil atu namakku vantu cēra villi" (tolkāppiyam : poruḷatikāram marapiyalil pala iṭic cerukalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu tolkāppiya aṟiñarkaḷ ti. cavarirāyaṉ, kā.cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi, cōmacuntara pārati, veḷḷivāraṇaṉ, ilakkuvaṉār, kamil cuvelapil, irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ, ce.vi. caṇmukam pōṉṟavar ēṟ ṟuḷḷatākum. kuṟippāka nālvaraṇam paṟṟik kūṟum marapiyal nūṟpākkaḷ 71-85 iṭic cerukal eṉpatu ivvaṟiñarkaḷum (ēṉ naṭuniliyāḷar aṉivarum) ēṟpatākum.

tolkāppiyam
7. tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari eṉpatip perumpālāṉa naṭunili aṟiñar ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. camaṟkirutattukku (pāṣikku) ilakkaṇam varinta pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭu. pāṇiṉikku muntiya camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. vaṭa intiyāviṟkuḷ nākarikamaṟṟa āriyar nuḻinta ki.mu.1500kku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil intiyāveṅkum tamiḻiya moḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟukku varivaṭivamum iruntatu. ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum iruntu avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum karuttukkaḷum irukku vētattilum ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu uṇmi. eṉavē tolkāppiyamō allatu ataṟku muntiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷō " eṉpa" "eṉmaṉār pulavar" pōṉṟavi ivaṟṟic cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa) pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭaviyāka irun tirukkalām eṉac cila tamiḻaṟiñar karutuvatu cāṉṟillāta kūṟṟaṉṟu. "tolkāppiyar eḻutiyataṉāl "tolkāppiyam" eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭu varum marapu. āyiṉum tolkāppiyam valla aṟiñarkaḷ, ilakkuvaṉār, ca.vē.cuppiramaṇiyaṉ, poṟkō, culevapil pōṉṟavarkaḷ "tol + kāppu + iyam" - tolkāppiyam, atāvatu toṉmarapik kākkum nūl eṉak kāraṇap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatum poruttamākavē uḷḷatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttu, col atikāraṅkaḷilum poruḷ atikāra akattiṇi, puṟattiṇi iyalkaḷilum cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭic cerukalkaḷ piṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatum poruḷatikārattiṉ iṟuti ēḻu iyalkaḷilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭiccerukal mikappala eṉpatum cuvelapil, mār pōṉṟōr karuttu. pirāmaṇiya ātikkam mikunta kālattil tolkāppiyam muḻumiyāka aḻittoḻikkappaṭāmal pātukāppataṟkeṉṟē tamiḻ, tamiḻar pāl paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷum cila iṭiccerukalkaḷi virakākac ceytirukkalām eṉpar pāvāṇar. (eṉiṉum tolkāppiya mutalurikārar iḷam pūraṇar kālamākiya ki.pi. 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarc ceytu muṭikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum) tolkāppiyam "māntaṉ aṟivāṟṟal evvaḷavu viyattaku ucca nili eytakkūṭum eṉpatik kāṭṭum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu' One of the finest monuments of human intelligence TUI eṉpar cuvelapil. ilakkiyak koḷkiyil vēṟu entap paṇṭiya moḻikaḷilum illāta ciṟanta karuttukkaḷit tol poruḷ. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟuvati cuvelapil (1991) kaṭṭuri viḷakkukiṟatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattil kaṭi āṟu iyalkaḷum puṇarcci paṟṟiyaṉa. "tamiḻil puṇarcci paṟṟiya ilakkaṇam taṉittaṉmi koṇṭatu. oṭṭunili viṭṭut taḷarātu, iṉṟaḷavum ''kaṉṉit tamiḻāka" viḷaṅkuvatu, ippuṇarcci paṟṟiya aṟiviyal aṇuku muṟikaḷiṉālēyām'' (tamiḻaṇṇa l, 2004)
tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyal kōṭpāṭṭuc ciṟappup paṟṟi cuvelepil (1974) kūṟiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
tolkāppiyam aṭippaṭi nūlāka iruppatu ilak kaṇa ilakkiya āyvukaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟu; mānta iṉap paraval cārnta puviyiyal, kumukāya māntaviyal, paṇpāṭu, cūḻniliyiyal, uḷaviyal ākiya pulaṅkaḷ cārnta ariya ceytikaḷ annūlil uḷḷaṉa ; eṉavē tamiḻar nākarikam - paṇpāṭu cārnta (ēṉ potuvāṉa mānta iṉa nākarikam - paṇpāṭu cārnta) āyvukaḷukkut tolkāppiyam mika iṉṟiyamiyātatu. eṉiṉum ilakkiyam kūṟuvatellām (akkālattil nilaviya) uṇmik kumukāya niliyi nēraṭiyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal tavaṟu; uṇmi niliyi viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi aṟiviyal pūrvamākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal mēlum tavaṟākum. akkāla āṇ peṇmakkaḷiṉ akam, puṟa vāḻkkiyi uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi tatrupamākac caṅkap pulavarkaḷuṭiya pāṭalkaḷ kāṭṭuvatākak kumuka varalāṟṟāciriyar cilar muṉṉar koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl uṇmi eṉṉa? ceyyuḷ 'uṇmi vāḻkki" nikaḻccikaḷi antanta nikaḻcci eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa ilakkiya marapu uḷḷatō anta marapukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē cittirikkiṟatu. atāvatu marapuk kēṟṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ cittirikkiṟatu. ''uṇmi vāḻkki " vēṟu; ati ilakkiyattil kāṭṭum marapu vēṟu; eṉpatip pulavarkaḷum poruḷilakkaṇavāciriyarum uṇarn tiruntaṉar. uṇarntiruntataṉāltāṉ uṇmi vāḻkkiyiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ ceykikaḷi 'ulakiyal vaḻakku" eṉṟum ilakkiyattil (ceyyuḷil) vāḻkkiyic cittirikkum marapic ceyyuḷ vaḻakku "pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku" eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar. cila nikaḻccikaḷ, ceytikaḷi ippaṭi ippaṭit tāṉ kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ilakkiyap paṭippāḷarkaḷum vācakarkaḷum tamakkuḷ icintu uruvākkum mēlvaric caṭṭamākiya marapē ilakkiya marapākum. (e.kā. ovvoru tiṇikkum uriya mutal, karu, uripporuḷkaḷ). nikaḻcci / ceyti vācakarukkut terintatu tāṉ; ataṉi oru putiya pārviyil, kōṇattil (marapi aṭippaṭiyil mīṟāmal) kāṭṭum poḻutu vācakar anta nayatti naṉku uṇarntu pārāṭṭa iyalkiṟatu. (itaṉi pi.marutanāyakam tamatu Poetics of the lyrics in classical languages" (Chemmozhi 1-2 Jan - March 2007) kaṭṭuriyil mēlum teḷivākkukiṟār) akappāṭal etuvum orutaṭavi kūṭat talivaṉ, talivi peyaric cuṭṭu vatilli. talivaṉum taliviyum eppoḻutum iṉṉār eṉṟu kuṟippāka aṭiyāḷam kāṭṭa muṭiyātavarkaḷē. eṉavē akappāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum uṇarvukaḷ (ṭapiḷyu. ec. haṭcaṉ tamatu "ilakkiyap payiṟcikkāṉa muṉṉuri' 1946 nūl pakkam 97 il colvatu pōla) "potuvāka enta āṭavaṉukkum peṇṇukkum poruntuvatāka irukku mēyaṉṟi ivaṉukkut tāṉ ivaḷukkuttāṉ poruntum eṉṟu irukkātu. (uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷi viḷakkātu) veḷiyulaki viritturikkum puṟappāṭalkaḷil cila potuvāka aṉivarum nōkkum pārviyil irukkum; cila antantap pulavaruṭiya taṉi nōkkil amintatāka irukkum. piṉṉavaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiyaṟunilip pāṭalkaḷ. vaḷḷalkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ iṟantāl pulavarkaḷ tam neñciṉ āḻattiliruntu uṇarvukaḷik koṭṭip pāṭum tuyarap pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉittaṉmi vāyntavi; potu vāṉa nāṭṭuppuṟa oppārikaḷiṉ vālāyap paṭimaṅkaḷik koṇṭaviyalla".
8. tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyattiṉ mukāmi nūl kaḷākiya eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ (vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta) ciṟappukaḷāka kamil cuvelapil, jārj lūcarṉ hārṭ, ēkē. rāmāṉucaṉ pi. maruta nāyakam ākiyōr terivittuḷḷa karuttukkaḷ cila varumāṟu. (innālvarumē kaḻaka ilakkiyatti āḻntu payiṉṟavarkaḷ; piṟa cemmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum terintavarkaḷ ; nuṇmāṇuḻipula mikkavarkaḷ) :
"eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu nūlkaḷ (mottam 2300 pāṭalkaḷ) acciṭṭu ulakiṉ pārvikku mutalil vantatu 1880 - 1910 kāla aḷavil ākum. avi veḷivanta akkaṇamē (atuvari eḷitāṉa piṟkāla intiya moḻikaḷil oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭa nili māṟi ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻ ākiviṭṭatu. pireñcu āciyaviyalaṟiñar piyar meyl "kirēkka uṇarccip pākkaḷil tali ciṟanta navamaṇikaḷukku iṇiyāṉavi iccaṅka ilakkiyap pāṭal kaḷ; intiyāvil, ēṉ ulakil, ilakkiyap paṭippukaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu caṅka ilakkiyam" eṉṟu ceytuḷḷa matippīṭṭi caṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷi āḻntu payi ṉṟuḷḷa evarum ēṟṟuk koḷvar.''
- cuvelapil (1960) 
"ulakiṉ nākarikam - paṇpāṭṭukkut tirāviṭarkaḷ, kuṟip pākat tamiḻarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa ciṟanta paṅkaḷippukaḷ pallava, cōḻar kālak kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭakkali ; cōḻar kālac ceppup paṭimaṅkaḷ ; parata nāṭṭiyam eṉa aḻikkappaṭum tamiḻar naṭaṉak kali ; karunāṭaka ici eṉa aḻikkappaṭum tamiḻ ici mutaliya palavām; avaṟṟuḷḷum talimi cāṉṟa ciṟappu vāyntatu (caṅka kālac cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyam. piṟamoḻi, piṟa nāṭṭu aṟiñar iti uṇarntu ivvilakki yattip payiṉṟu ulaka mānta iṉattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta uyariya marapuc celvamāka makiḻntu pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. uccanilikku muḻut takutiyuṭiya avvilakkiyattiṟku itu vari annili tarappaṭavilli .''
- cuvelapil (1973) 
"intiyāviṉ piṟa enta moḻi ilakkiyattilum illāta ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷik koṇṭatu eṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟip paṇṭit tamiḻ (caṅka) ilakkiyam uyariya ilakkiya nayamum vāyntatu. ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷ variciyil tamiḻukkuriya iṭattik kaḻaka ilakkiyattiṉ 26350 varikaḷ koṇṭa pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟut tantuviṭṭaṉa. av vilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappi ippoḻututāṉ ulaku uṇarat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.''
''aci, cīr, taḷi aṭippaṭiyilāṉa yāppiyal, akam puṟak kōṭpāṭu; uvamaviyal, ākiya aṉittilum taṉit taṉmi vāynta ciṟanta ilakkiyak koḷki koṇṭa kaḻaka nūlkaḷi uruvākkiya perumi tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭukku maṭṭumē uriya ciṟappākum.
"intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta cevviyal ilakkiyam - cevviyal moḻip perum paṇpāṭukaḷ iraṇṭu - oṉṟu camaṟkirutam; maṟṟatu tamiḻ ; avi oṉṟi oṉṟu cārntavi yalla - taṟcārpāṉavi.
"intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyē tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē cevvilakkiyamākavum, nikaḻkāla ilakkiyamākavum orē nērattil ilaṅkum ciṟappu vāyntatu; atu camaṟkirutac cevviyal ilakkiyam aḷavu toṉmi vāyntatu; paṇṭi kirēkka moḻic ceyyuḷkaḷi eppaṭic cevvilakkiyam eṉa ulaku karutukiṟatō atē taṉmi vāyntatu tamiḻk kaḻaka ilakkiyam. uyirt tuṭippuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uruvākum moḻiyākavum tamiḻ uḷḷatu.

"tamiḻc cevvilakkiyam (caṅka ilakkiyam) makkaḷip paṟṟiya ilakkiyam ; makkaḷ uruvākkiyatu - āṉāl "nāṭṭup puṟa ilakkiyamaṉṟu"
- cuvelapil (1973) 
''vāḻkki iṉiyatu; makiḻcci taruvatu. cāvu el lōrukkum varuvatu. ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu viṭutali peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyellām illi. vaḷḷaṇmi yum naṉmiyum iyalpāka amikiṉṟaṉa - ātaṉiya (āṉmīka) nalaṉukkākavō kaḻuvāyākavō alla. iṟantapiṉṉar vīrarum cāṉṟōrum tuṟakkattil niliyāka vāḻvar; tīyōr mīḷā nirayam eytuvar eṉak karutiṉar. nila ulakil pukaḻi niṟīic ceṉṟavar tuṟakkattil iṉiya vāḻvu eṉṉum vekumati peṟuvar : kātal, puṇarcci, tirumaṇam, makkaṭpēṟu ivi mānta vāḻvil niṟivu peṟuvataṟkāṉa vaḻimuṟikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷḷaṉmi, vīracceyal, piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉāka vāḻtal - ivaṟṟil oruvaṉ celaviṭṭa nāṭkaḷi niṉittup pulavarum piṟarum kūṟum pukaḻ moḻikaḷ maṭṭumē oruvaṉ cāvukkup piṉṉar niliniṟpaviyākum.''
- cuvelapil (1974) 
''kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ekkālattukkum poruntum aḻakiyal kūṟukaḷ, viḻumaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi nirampak koṇ ṭavi. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar yātta avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil (atāvatu aṇmikkālat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar) vakuttuḷḷa ilakkiya nayakkōṭpāṭukaḷiṉ paṭiyum uyar niliyil ēṟkappaṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
''kaḻaka ilakkiyattip payilum oru kumukāyamē (taṉinapar maṭṭumalla) avvilakkiya viḻumaṅkaḷi eyta iyalum vakiyil, eyta viḻiyum vakiyil avvilakkiyam amin tuḷḷatu. eṉavē tāṉ ikkāla ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvāḷarum pōṟṟum vakiyil ulaka māntar aṉivarum matikkat takka eṉṟu muḷ (cirañcīvi)ttaṉmi koṇṭuḷḷatu kaḻaka ilakkiyam''
- cuvelapil (1974) 

"paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyamum paṇpāṭum paṭitta akam - puṟakkōṭpāṭukaḷ ulakilēyē taṉittaṉmiyum ciṟap pum vāyntavi. cila poruṇmikaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattukkum camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattukkum oppumi kāṇappaṭalām. paṭṭāl eṉṉa? camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiliruntu tāṉ tamiḻ ilakkiyam kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkum eṉa ēṉ karuta vēṇṭum? (nērmāṟākavum nikaḻntirukkalām aṉṟō ?)"
- cuvelapil (1975) 
"potuvāṉa ceytiyit teḷivāka, muḻumiyāṉa nuṇṇiya pārviyil nōkki, eḷiya āṉāl vayiram pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil, āḻnta poruḷuṭaṉ taruvatē ciṟanta pāṭal eṉa ikkālattil karutappaṭukiṟatu. veḷippaṭiyāṉa orē ceytiyit taruvativiṭa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa pala poruḷit taravallatāṉa coṟceṭṭumikka pāṭalē ciṟanta pālat taṉmiyuṭiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tolkāppiyam nōkku, payaṉ, eccam (colleccam, kuṟippeccam) uḷḷuṟi, iṟicci eṉṟu kūṟuvatellām itu tāṉ ;
- pi. marutanāyakam (2007) 
9. iṟutiyākac cevviyal moḻikaḷāṉa tamiḻ, camaṟkirutam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉ ākiya nāṉkum terintavarum - am moḻikaḷiṉ cevviyal ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟavarumāṉa jārj lūcarṉ hārṭ tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpataṟkāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷip piṉvarumāṟu 2003il ōr aṟikkiyil terivittuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu :
''mutalāvatāka, tamiḻiṉ toṉmi kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ikkālattiya piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ōrāyiram āṇṭu muṉṉar uruvāṉa ilakkiyattik koṇṭatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉ āti nūl ākiya tolkāppiyattiṉ pakutikaḷ ki.mu. 200ai oṭṭiyavi eṉpatu mikap paḻiya (tamiḻil) kal veṭṭukaḷiliruntu terikiṟatu. paḻantamiḻp perunūlkaḷāṉa eṭṭuttoki pattuppāṭṭu mutaliyavi ki.pi oṉṟu - iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷic cārntavi. intiyāviṉ miku toṉmi vāynta , camayam cārāta (secular) pāṭalkaḷ aviyē; kāḷitācaṉ ilacciyaṅkaḷukku irunūṟu āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭavi.''
"iraṇṭāvatāka, tamiḻ ilakkiya marapu maṭṭum tāṉ (camaṟkirutattiliruntu peṟappaṭāta) intiya maṇṇukkuriya ilakkiya marapu ākum. teṉṉāṭṭil camaṟkirutat tākkam valuppaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē mukiḻttatu atu. camaṟkiruta piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉmi yuṭiyatu atu. tamiḻ taṉakkeṉṟu taṉi ilakkiyak koḷki, ilakkaṇa marapu, murukiyal (aesthetics) uṭiyatu; ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aminta tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam vēṟu emmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta taṉittaṉmi vāyntatu (unique). camaṟkirutattilō vēṟu intiya moḻi kaḷilō uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa uṇarvi (sensibility) - intiya maṇṇukkē uriya uṇarvip paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil kāṇalām; tamiḻppulavarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅkālam vaḷartteṭutta ceḻumiyāṉa uṇarvu marapunili atu.
"mūṉṟāvatāka, camaṟkirutam - ilattīṉ, cīṉam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟukku iṇiyāṉa taram vāyntatu tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam. avvilakkiyam nuṇmāṇuḻipulamikkatu; palvēṟu pāṭu poruḷ koṇṭatu (muṟkāla intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil potu makkaḷip paṟṟiyum niṟiyap pāṭiyatu tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē); mānta iṉattukkup potumiyāṉa viḻumiyaṅkaḷik kūṟiyatu. eṉavē piṟa cevviyal moḻikaḷiṉ marapukaḷuṭaṉum ilakkiyaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum camamāka niṟkum uraṉ vāyntatu atu. ulakiṉ taliciṟanta aṟanūlkaḷil oṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ. tamiḻc cevviyal ilakkiyattil atu pōṉṟu pala tuṟikaḷilum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. avvilakkiyam pāṭit tulakkam tarāta mānta iṉa paṭṭaṟivu oṉṟum illi.
"iṟutiyāka ikkāla intiyap paṇpāṭṭiyum marapiyum uṇarva taṟkup payila vēṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻilakkiyam mutaṉmi vāyntatu. camaṟkiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil teṉṉaka maraput tākkam uḷḷatu kuṟittu nāṉ virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. kaḻakakkālam toṭaṅkit tamiḻilil pāṭappaṭṭa ciṟanta intu camaya patti ilakkiya nūlkaḷ tām taṟpōtiya intu camayattukku aṭittaḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ patti ilakkiyak karuttukaḷ pākavata purāṇattilum piṟa (teluṅku kaṉṉapcamaṟkiruta) patti nūlkaḷilum ēṟṟappaṭṭu intiyā muḻuvatum paraviṉa. vētattukkuc camamākak karutap paṭum tamiḻ (tivyapirapantap patti ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil periya tirumāl kōyilkaḷil (tiruppati pōṉṟavi) vētattōṭu cērttu ōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāla intō āriya moḻikaḷukku camaṟkirutamē mūlamoḻi; atu pōla ikkālat tamiḻukkum maliyāḷattukkum cevviyal tamiḻē mūla moḻiyākum.
''ulakattiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil tamiḻum oṉṟu eṉpatu ittuṟiyil valla aṟiñarukku uḷḷaṅki nellik kaṉi pōlat terinta uṇmiyākum" 
caṅka kāla akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ
10. caṅka ilakkiya akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappi ē. kē. rāmāṉucaṉ (1969) pārāṭṭiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
"toṉmiyāṉaviyē eṉiṉum iṉṟum uyirūṭṭam uḷḷaviyāy ilaṅkum inta (caṅka ilakkiya) akap pāṭal kaḷip pōṉṟa nayam vāyntavi intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu evaṟṟilum illi. appāṭalkaḷ kūṟum vāḻkki neṟi yilum, viḷakkum nikaḻccikaḷilum ciṟanta cemmoḻi ilak kiyattiṉ piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa : kātalōṭu kaṉi vum paṇpāṭum ; veḷippaṭik kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu uḷḷuṟi, iṟicci, aṅkatam ākiyaviyum; talivaṉ, talivi peyar cuṭṭappaṭāviṭiṉum ōviyam pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya vaṇṇaṉi; aṭikaḷ cila avi cuṭṭum poruḷō peritu. tamiḻar aṟivuttiṟaṉiṉ mikat toṉmiyāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu iv vakappāṭalkaḷ ; atu maṭṭumaṉṟu kaṭanta īrāyiram āṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyap paṭippil ivaṟṟi viñcuvaṉa illi."
11. akap pāṭalkaḷiṉ amippup paṟṟi cuvelapil (1986) viḷakkamum karuttiṟ koḷḷat takkatu.
"akappāṭal ovvoṉṟum oru kūṟṟu : talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliya cilaruḷ oruvar, nāṭaka māntar pōl, kūṟuvatāka atu amiyum. avarkaḷ oruvarukkoruvarō, taṉakkut tāṉākavō, nilavi nōkkiyō kūṟuvati vācakar kaḷākiya nām kēṭkiṟōm; avvaḷavē. nāṭakak kāṭci oṉṟi muḻumiyāka akappāṭal oṉṟil uḷḷa kūṟṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭiviṭum. ivi nāṭṭuppāṭalkaḷ pōl virintu pāṭiyavi alla; oru nikaḻcciyik kaṇṭa allatu eṇṇip pārtta oru pāvalar atu paṟṟiya tam eṇṇaṅkaḷi nērākavō urumāṟṟiyō veḷippaṭuttiya pāṭalkaḷum - alla. ivi aracavippāṭalkaḷiṉ naḷiṉam uṭiyavi; ''vikalum eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ tiṅkaḷ valitta kāl pōl" nuṇṇiya pulamittiṟaṉuṭaṉ yākkappaṭṭavi.
12. mēṟkaṇṭa iruvar kūṟṟukkaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ varumāṟu:
“In their antiquity and in their contemporaniety, there is not much else in any Indian literature equal to these quiet and dramatic Tamil poems. In their values and stances they represent a mature classical poetry : passion is balanced by courtesy, transparency by ironies and nuances of design, impersonality by vivid detil, leanness of line by richness of implication. These poems are not just the earliest evidence of the Tamil genius. The Tamils in all their 2000 years of literary effort wrote nothing better”. - A.K. Ramanujan.
An Aham poem is always a “Kurru”, an utterance by one of the limited number of dramatis personale, (hero, heroine, heroine's girl-friend, foster mother ete). The reader only overhears what the characters say to each other, to themselves, or to the moon. A poem in this tradition implies, evokes, enacts a drama in a monologue. The poems are not the result of rapid composition like oral epics or as lyrical expessions or sublimations of the poet's own feelings triggered by some romantic event. They are the result of skillful and subtle care, the sophistication of court poetry, “like a chariot wheel made thoughtfully over a month by a carpenter who tosses off eight chariots in a day.'' - K.V. Zvelebil.
puṟappāṭalkaḷ :
13. puṟanāṉūṟu : innūlil iṉṟu kiṭṭiyuḷḷa 368 pāṭalkaḷiṉ vakippāṭu varumāṟu : cērar - 22; cōḻar - 61; pāṇṭiyar - 31; kuṟunila maṉṉar -134; piṟa -120. hārṭ (1999) ''āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyāvi, ēṉ perumaḷavukku āriyarukku muntiya intiyāviyē viḷakkik kāṭṭum āvaṇam puṟanāṉūṟu eṉpar" - a testament of pre-Aryan South India and, to a significant extent, of preAryan India. "avarum hāṅk hipeṭs - m cērntu 1999 il puṟanāṉūṟu muḻuvatiyumma āṅkilattil pāṭalkaḷāka pānayattuṭaṉ moḻipeyart tuḷḷa ṉar : - "The four hundred Songs of war and wisdom'' (Columbia universitiy Press; New york).

patiṟṟuppattu : innūlil mutal pattum iṟutip pattum tavira eṭṭuppattukaḷic cārnta 80 pāṭalkaḷē iṉṟu kiṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ pala iruppiṉum cēra maṉṉarip pukaḻvataṟkeṉṟē eḻutiya ippāṭalkaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷa ciṟanta pānayam vāynta pāṭalkaḷiṉ taram uṭiyaṉavāka illi. patiṟṟuppattu muḻuvatiyum āṅkilattil urinaṭiyil peyarttuḷḷavar ē.vi. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ (1980). 
tirukkuṟaḷ -
14. ''aṟitōraṟiyāmi kaṇṭaṟṟāl" eṉpatu tiruk kuṟaḷukkum poruntuvatākum. kuṟaḷiṉ ciṟappukkaḷip pārāṭṭip pala moḻi, pala nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟit tokuttāl atuvē oru periya nūlākiviṭum! ulakap periyārkaḷil oruvarāṉa ālperṭ cuviṭcar 1936il veḷiyiṭṭa "intiyac cintaṉiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyum" (Indian Thought and its Development; Hodder and Stoughton; London; PP. 272) eṉṉum nūliṉ 200 - 205 pakkaṅkaḷil intiya meyyiyal aṟivarkaḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar taliciṟantu viḷaṅku vatip pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. cila pakutikaḷ varumāṟu:
"intiya makkaḷiṭiyē paṇṭik kālattiliruntē piṟa uyirkaḷ aṉittukkum aṉpu kāṭṭi utavum aṟakkoḷki (the idea of active love) iruntu vantati, intiyap paṇṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇum katikaḷil iruntu aṟikiṟōm. attuṭaṉ kuṟippākat tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūlil kāṇum aṟavuric ceyyuḷkaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm.
'	'(ulakil piṟa uyirkaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭic ceyal paṭuvati (life Affirmation) avvaḷavāka valiyuṟuttāta maṉu tarma cāttiram pōṉṟavaṟṟi liruntu māṟupaṭum) kuṟaḷil ulakiyum vāḻkkiyiyum veṟuttu otukkum pōkku (World and life negation) paravalākak kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟu. eṅkō ōriru iṭattil vēṇṭumāṉāl irukkalām.
''pirāmaṇīyam, puttamatam, pakavatkīti pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōl immi maṟumip payaṉ karutiyē aṟaneṟiyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vāṇikak koḷkiyik kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttu vatu illi. nallatu ceyvatē takkatu eṉṟa uṇarviṉā lēyē nallatu (aṟam) ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kuṟaḷ kūṟukiṟatu (kuṟaḷkaḷ 222, 211). pakavatkītiyō karumam ceytu koṇ ṭiruppatu pirapañcaniyati eṉṟu vaṟaṭṭuttaṉamāka valintu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, eṉṉa viyappu, kuṟaḷō māntaṉ uḻip patum īṭṭuvatum piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum avaṉ naṉmi ceyvataṟkākattāṉ eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṟatu. (kuṟaḷkaḷ 81,212 ) pakavat kītippaṭi kaṭami cātikkut takkapaṭi vēṟupaṭum kuṟaḷō makkaḷ aṉivarum 'nallavi ceytoḻukak kūṟukiṟatu.''
''ēṉi intiya tarma cāttirakkārar uḻippatil, muyaṟci ceyvatil makiḻvōṭu īṭupaṭu eṉṟu kūṟavilli . kuṟaḷkaḷ 619, 630, pōṉṟaviyē ulakiyalilum, vāḻkkiyilum, ārvat tuṭaṉ īṭupaṭumpaṭi aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
"(puttamum kītiyum pōlap paṟṟiṉmi, veṟup piṉmi, kollāmi, akimci ākiyavaṟṟiyum kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttiṉālum kūṭa atē camayattil) kuṟaḷ atikamāka valiyuṟuttuvatu aṉpum aruḷumēyām (the living ethic of love). kāṇka kuṟaḷkaḷ 72, 78, 79, 103,226, 241.
"aṟaneṟicār ulaka vāḻkki ilaṭciyattit tiṟam paṭac cuṭṭukiṟatu kuṟaḷ.māntaṉiṉ taṉi vāḻkki , piṟa uyir kaḷōṭum ulakattōṭum uṟaḻum avaṉ vāḻkki ākiya vaṟṟil kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya neṟikaḷik kuṟaḷ viyattaku paṇpuṭaṉum cālpuṭaṉum naṭimuṟikkukanta vakiyil vakut tuḷḷatu. (Characterised by nobility and good sense). ulaka ilakkiyattil vēṟu eṅkum ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oḷimayamāṉa aṟavuri vācakaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkum nūlik kāṇa iyalātu. (kuṟaḷka ḷ 92, 105, 108, 121,159, 162, 216,298,319,578,594,628,757, 782, 874,931,973, 999, 1007,1024,1032)".
"āka, kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkak kālattilēyē intiya makkaḷiṭiyē ulakavāḻkkiyiṭamum uyirkaḷiṭamum paṇpum aṉpum kāṭṭum oru taṉmi iyalpāka iruntu vantuḷḷatik kuṟaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pirāmaṇiyam, puttam, pakavatkīti cār intumatam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷil illātatu atu. āṉāl kīḻcāti yiṉariṭamum, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṭamum tōṉṟi makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāka vāḻnta ciṟanta camayap pōtakarkaḷ mūlamāka attaṉmi metumetuvāka nāḷaṭivil intu matattil iṭam peṟalāyiṟṟu".
15. kirāliṉ tirukkuṟaḷ cerumaṉ moḻipeyarppip paṭittuviṭṭuttāṉ ālparṭ cuviṭcar (1936) mēṟkaṇṭa vāṟu viyantu pārāṭṭiṉār; "Free Hindusthan" eṉṟa peyaril tārakanāt tās (1884-1958) amerikkāvil naṭattiya itaḻukku tālstāyiṭam oru kaṭṭuri kēṭṭār. tācukku 14-12-1908 aṉṟu tālstāy oru kaṭṭuri aṉuppiṉār; "māntar vāḻkkikkut tēviyāṉa orē viti aṉpuṭimi ; aṉ puṭimikkuk kaṭṭaḷikkal tīyavi ceytārkkum iṉṉā ceyyāmiyēyākum" [Great religious teachers have lid down) one invariable condition of love, namely the enduring of injuries, insults and violence of all kinds without resisting evil by evil. (intiyar palarum maṟupiṟaviyi (reincarnation) nampuvatāl mēṟkaṇṭa vācakattil reincarnation ai nīkkiviṭa tālstāyiṭam aṉumati kēṭṭār kānti ; " nīkkavēṇṭām, eṉpatu eṉ karuttu eṉiṉum tāṅkaḷ virumpiṉāl nīkkikkoḷka'' eṉṟu tālstāy maṟu moḻiviṭuttār 7-10-1909 aṉṟu aṉumati vantavuṭaṉ tālstāyiṉ 14-12-1908 kaṭitattik kāntiyaṭikaḷ acciṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkil parappiṉār. icceytiyiṉ virivip piṉvarum nūlil kāṇalām :
